```
## Summary

### 1. Timeline of Major Design Decisions and Features (Level 1)

**June 3** — Repository began as `signalkit`, a Python telemetry CLI (commits e9326c6 through f2257f8).

**June 5** — **Pivot to Planetfall.** Complete rewrite: shifted from CLI to a 3D Three.js web game. Built the core procedural world:
  - Lavender ocean planet with gold metallic islands (PBR materials)
  - Saturn-style banded ring with proper gaps and transparency
  - Soft atmosphere + starfield + drifting clouds
  - Dual-view architecture: orbit camera + island walkable terrain
  - First-person movement controller on procedurally-generated terrain
  - Checked out the two-level flow design (orbit → crossfade → island)
  - Established visual palette and lighting (soft, dreamy, calm—not "busy neural" aesthetics)

**June 5–6** — Added music, refined landing marker visuals, and entry mechanics.

**June 9, ~1:39 AM (28 checkpoints in session `a8d5fde0be94`, 27.9M tokens)** — **The big design session.** Rizel searched real Entire CLI source (`~/Documents/work/cli`), recapped the project evolution, and brainstormed the core Level 1 game loop. **Key decision: Diner Dash-inspired rhythm.** Settled on:
  - **The three-beat Diner Dash loop:** `git add` (stage/recover) → `git commit` (freeze in ice) → `[y/n] link to checkpoint?` (bank progress)
  - **Single level-wide countdown (45 seconds)** with escalating pressure: urgent red under 22s, critical frantic pulse + screen shake under 8s
  - **Panic sky:** lavender dusk lerps toward crimson starting at ~22s, reading barely at first, fully red + pulsing in final 10s
  - **Melt mechanic:** unbanked memories (frozen but not checkpointed) sink and fade when time runs out; triggers a "MEMORIES MELTED" fail screen and a R-to-retry loop
  - **Ice block visual:** commit encases memory in translucent blue crystal; glows when checkpointed
  - **Real checkpoint cards:** floating 12-char hex ID (e.g. `711044b1fe29`), `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>` trailer stamp, ship power bar, summary
  - **Terminal scaffold:** walk up to memory → ship terminal opens → `git add`, `git commit`, y/n confirmation, then `entire checkpoint list` at the end to review all banked checkpoints
  - **Diegetic teaching:** wrong commands get nudged ("command not recognized — try: <cmd>")
  - **Final beat:** `entire checkpoint list` is the true finish line; clock keeps running through it (you don't win until you type it before time expires)

**June 9, ~2:18 AM (checkpoint `b7da40df6cf3`)** — Implemented the full loop in code (`src/islandView.js`, ~800 lines):
  - Landing briefing modal (story frame + controls instructions: arrows/WASD + mouse look + E to inspect + B to leave)
  - Countdown HUD + ship power meter + "Press E" proximity hints
  - Three memory artifacts placed on the terrain with light beams
  - Full state machine: dormant → recovered (git add) → frozen (git commit) → linked (checkpoint y/n) → banked
  - Real command validation + command line UI

**June 9, 5:35 AM to 5:51 AM (checkpoints `b7da40df6cf3` and `4f7bddd23553`)** — Polish pass:
  - Removed "signal lost · reconstructing memory" subtitle (it didn't add to the narrative)
  - Tightened HUD stack (no floating gap below countdown)
  - Added "CONTROLS" section to briefing modal (clarified E, B, arrow keys as primary controls; WASD as alternative)
  - Pushed all changes to main branch

### 2. Level 2 Ideas & Deferred Features (from session `f19355bf1859`)

**Immediate Level 2 concept** (your own prompt, 2026-06-09 05:39):

The **rewind payoff** — explicitly saved for L2. In L1 you *banked* checkpoints; L2 teaches you to **use one to undo a disaster.**

**Fiction:** A corrupted memory from L1 is spreading and breaking ship systems. Player must:
  1. Find the corruption's origin
  2. Search for the last *good* checkpoint before corruption started (teaches `entire checkpoint search "..."`)
  3. Explain what that checkpoint contained (teaches `entire checkpoint explain <id>`)
  4. Rewind the ship to that checkpoint (teaches `entire checkpoint rewind --to <id>`)

This teaches three more authentic Entire commands while delivering the "checkpoints aren't just saved—they let you go back" moment that makes the system feel like a superpower.

**Design note in PLAN.md (pre-L1, deferred for later):**
- **Juggling multiple memories** under tighter timers (goes beyond calm tutorial mode)
- **Sessions concept:** multiple checkpoints belonging to one session (Entire CLI feature not yet taught)
- **Recap, activity, dispatch:** full command suite for more islands/levels
- **More islands:** currently only one island is built; orbit shows single pin
- **Polish backlog:** bloom on gold glints, ring shadow on planet, island landmarks/props (currently feels like empty dunes), water shader refinement, proper skybox, third-person option

### 3. Entire Concepts/Commands Taught vs. Not Taught

**Level 1 teaches (real commands, diegetically in-game):**
- `git add` — stage/recover a memory
- `git commit` — freeze a change; commit trailer gets stamped with `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>`
- Checkpoint linking (the `[y/n]` confirmation after commit; in the real CLI this happens automatically via post-commit hook, but the game makes it explicit as a teaching beat)
- `entire checkpoint list` — review all banked checkpoints; the finish line of the level

**Level 1 does NOT teach (deferred to later levels):**
- `entire checkpoint rewind --to <id>` — go back in time (Level 2 main mechanic)
- `entire checkpoint search "..."` — find a checkpoint by keyword
- `entire checkpoint explain <id>` — inspect a checkpoint's metadata
- `recap`, `activity`, `dispatch` — other context-recovery commands (planned as later puzzles per PLAN.md)
- Sessions (`entire session resume/attach`) — multiple checkpoints grouped into one session
- The hook system — real Entire checkpoints are created automatically post-commit; L1's y/n beat is an invented decision moment

**Important accuracy note:** The real CLI does NOT have a y/n prompt. Checkpoints are created automatically by the post-commit hook. The game's y/n beat is pedagogical scaffolding—it teaches "linking is a choice you make" when the product is actually "it's automatic." One design option for later: keep y/n in L1 as training wheels, then have a later level reveal the hooks doing it for you.
```

```
## Complete Entire CLI Command Inventory

Based on my exploration of the Entire CLI repository, here's the comprehensive inventory organized for your Level 2 game design:

### 1. COMPLETE LIST OF USER-FACING COMMANDS AND SUBCOMMANDS

#### **NOUN GROUPS (Canonical Command Structure)**

**Session Management Group** (`entire session` | alias: `sessions`)
- `list` — List all sessions tracked by Entire, including ended sessions
- `info <session-id>` — Show detailed session information (agent, model, status, worktree, timing, token usage, files touched)
- `stop [session-id]` — Stop one or more active sessions
- `current` — Show the active session for the current worktree
- `attach <session-id>` — Attach an existing agent session (link untracked sessions)
- `resume <branch>` — Switch to a branch and resume its session

**Checkpoint Management Group** (`entire checkpoint` | aliases: `cp`, `checkpoints`)
- `list` — List checkpoints on the current branch (with optional `--session` filter)
- `explain [checkpoint-id | commit-sha]` — Explain a session, commit, or checkpoint (with `--generate` for AI summaries)
- `rewind` — Browse checkpoints and rewind your session to a previous state
- `search [query]` — Search checkpoints using semantic and keyword matching

**Agent Management Group** (`entire agent`)
- `list` — List installed and available agents
- `add <agent-name>` — Install hooks for an agent (claude-code, codex, gemini, opencode, cursor, factoryai-droid, copilot-cli, pi)
- `remove <agent-name>` — Uninstall hooks for an agent

**Authentication Group** (`entire auth`)
- `login` — Log in to Entire (device auth flow)
- `logout` — Log out of Entire
- `status` — Show authentication status (identity, active context, active sessions)
- `contexts` — Show and manage authentication contexts
- `use <context>` — Switch the active login context

**Doctor/Diagnostics Group** (`entire doctor`)
- `doctor` — Diagnose and fix session issues (interactive scan-and-fix flow)
- `trace` — Show hook performance traces
- `logs` — Show recent operational logs
- `bundle` — Produce a diagnostic bundle (zip) for bug reports (secrets redacted by default)

**Labs/Experimental Group** (`entire labs`)
- `labs` — Explore experimental Entire workflows (discovery interface)
- Accessible from labs: `review` (code review with skills), `investigate` (multi-agent investigation loop), `recap` (activity summarization)

**Plugin Management Group** (`entire plugin`)
- `list` — List installed plugins
- `install <name>` — Install a plugin
- `remove <name>` — Remove a plugin

#### **TOP-LEVEL LIFECYCLE COMMANDS**

- `enable` — Enable Entire in current repository (with interactive setup for first-time)
- `disable` — Disable Entire integrations in the current repository
- `configure` — Update non-agent Entire settings (telemetry, git hooks, strategy options, summary provider)
- `status` — Show Entire status (enabled/disabled, active session, hooks installed, agents)
- `clean` — Clean up Entire session data (current head, all, orphaned data)
- `version` — Show build information

#### **ACTIVITY AND ANALYSIS COMMANDS**

- `activity` — Show your activity overview (TUI dashboard of recent sessions)
- `dispatch` — Generate a dispatch summarizing recent agent work
- `recap` — Summarize recent checkpoint activity (interactive TUI)

#### **ADVANCED/INFRASTRUCTURE COMMANDS (Hidden or Experimental)**

**Control Plane** (hidden during maturation):
- `org` — Manage Entire organizations (create, list)
- `project` — Manage Entire projects (create, list)
- `repo` — Manage Entire repositories (create, get, list, delete)
- `grant` — Manage Entire access grants and org membership
- `repo mirror` — Manage GitHub-mirror placements on EntireDB clusters

**Review Command** (hidden during maturation):
- `entire review` (accessed via `entire labs review`) — Run configured review skills inside an agent session with multi-agent support and synthesis

**Investigation Command** (hidden during maturation):
- `entire investigate` — Experimental multi-agent investigation loop

**Deprecated/Legacy**:
- `reset` — Reset the shadow branch and session state for current HEAD (deprecated in favor of `clean`)
- `trail` — Manage trails for your branches (hidden infrastructure)
- `trail watch [<number>]` — Tail a trail's code-review events live

#### **HIDDEN SHORTCUTS (Functional but deprecated)**

- `rewind` → `entire checkpoint rewind` 
- `resume` → `entire session resume`
- `attach` → `entire session attach`
- `explain` → `entire checkpoint explain`
- `trace` → `entire doctor trace`
- `search` — Hidden without hint (equivalent to `checkpoint search`)

---

### 2. CORE DAILY LOOP vs. ADVANCED/OCCASIONAL

**CORE DAILY LOOP (Everyday Operations)**
- `entire enable` — One-time setup per repository
- `entire agent add/remove` — Manage which agents to track
- `entire status` — Check current session state
- `entire checkpoint list` — Browse recent work snapshots
- `entire checkpoint rewind` — Recover from mistakes
- `entire session resume <branch>` — Continue work from another branch
- `entire session stop` — End sessions gracefully

**ADVANCED/OCCASIONAL (Power User)**
- `entire checkpoint explain` — Understand why a change was made (with `--generate` for AI summaries)
- `entire checkpoint search` — Find specific work by semantic/keyword search
- `entire session info <id>` — Deep inspection of a session
- `entire activity` — Overview dashboard of all recent activity
- `entire dispatch` — Create dispatch summaries for coordination
- `entire recap` — Summarize activity ranges
- `entire doctor` — Troubleshoot stuck sessions
- `entire clean --all` — Repository-wide cleanup
- `entire configure` — Adjust settings (telemetry, checkpoint remote, auto-summarization)

**EXPERIMENTAL/LABS**
- `entire labs review` — Code review with skill-based findings
- `entire investigate` — Multi-agent investigation loop
- Control-plane commands (org/project/repo/grant management)

---

### 3. INTERESTING CONCEPTS & TEACHABLE GAME MECHANICS

**Core Mental Models:**

1. **Checkpoints vs. Commits** — Checkpoints are "save points" within a session (created when code is committed). They include prompts, responses, files touched, token usage, and transcript. Commits stay clean on your branch; metadata lives separately on `entire/checkpoints/v1` shadow branch.

2. **Sessions** — Complete agent interactions from start to finish. Session ID format: `YYYY-MM-DD-<UUID>`. Can span multiple checkpoints. Can be "active" (in progress), "ended", or "stale" (no recent activity).

3. **Shadow Branches** — Temporary, worktree-specific branches (e.g., `entire/<commit-hash[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`) that track session state without polluting your commit history. Automatically cleaned up.

4. **Manual-Commit Strategy** — Entire never creates commits on your active branch. Safe on main/master. Session metadata is condensed to permanent checkpoints when you commit.

5. **Concurrent Sessions** — Multiple AI sessions can run on the same commit. Each gets its own checkpoint linkage; you can rewind to any of them independently.

6. **Checkpoint Linkage** — Checkpoints are linked to commits via trailers (`Entire-Checkpoint: <id>`). Survives rebase/amend via `post-rewrite` hook.

7. **Transcript Redaction** — Automatic best-effort secret redaction (API keys, tokens, credentials) when writing to `entire/checkpoints/v1`. Temporary shadow branches may contain unredacted data.

8. **Strategies** — Currently implements manual-commit strategy. Configurable via `strategy_options` in settings (e.g., `push_sessions`, `checkpoint_remote`).

**Teachable Game Mechanics:**

- **Rewind/Restore Loops** — Time-travel to any checkpoint and continue from there
- **Session Branching** — Multiple sessions on the same commit, track independently
- **Checkpoint Metadata** — See the "why" behind changes (transcript, prompts, token counts)
- **Worktree Isolation** — Each worktree has independent session tracking; no conflicts
- **Skill Events** — Capture which AI skills/tools were used (Claude, Gemini, Pi)
- **Transcript Persistence** — Full session logs searchable and explainable
- **Settings Priority** — Local settings override project settings (per-field override model)
```

Both scouts are back. Here's where we stand and my pitch for Level 2.

## What the history says

Two things lined up nicely. First, past-you already seeded the Level 2 concept in a checkpoint from this morning: the **rewind payoff** — Level 1 was about *banking* checkpoints, Level 2 is about *using* one to undo a disaster. Second, the real CLI has exactly three commands that form a natural arc for that story:

- `entire checkpoint search "..."` — semantic/keyword search across checkpoints
- `entire checkpoint explain <id>` — see what a checkpoint contained and why
- `entire checkpoint rewind` — browse checkpoints and restore to one (in the real CLI this opens an interactive browser, which is actually great for a game terminal)

## Level 2 pitch: "The Corruption"

**Fiction:** You return to the island from Level 1 — but one of the memories you banked has corrupted, and it's spreading. Black glitch-veins are creeping across the terrain, eating the artifacts you saved. You can't fix it forward; you have to go *back*.

Where Level 1 was Diner Dash (rhythm, forward pressure, beat the clock), Level 2 is an **escape room / detective inversion** — same terminal, same island, completely different verb:

1. **Investigate** — walk to glitched artifacts and inspect them (E, reusing L1's interaction) to collect clue fragments: keywords describing what the lost-good state contained. This gives the player the *vocabulary* they'll need to search with.
2. **Search** — at the terminal, `entire checkpoint search "navigation core"` returns a ranked list of checkpoint IDs. Wrong or vague queries return too many hits — teaches that search is semantic and that better queries narrow results.
3. **Verify** — `entire checkpoint explain <id>` shows the checkpoint's summary, files touched, and timestamp. The puzzle: two candidates look similar, but one is from *after* the corruption began. The clues from step 1 disambiguate.
4. **Rewind** — `entire checkpoint rewind` opens the browser (authentic to the real TUI), player arrows to the verified checkpoint, confirms — and the world visibly rewinds: corruption retracts, veins recede, sky clears. That's the spectacle moment and the product's whole pitch made physical: *checkpoints aren't a logbook, they're a time machine.*

**Pressure mechanic:** instead of a countdown, the corruption **spreads spatially** — it slowly consumes terrain and artifacts, and if it reaches the ship terminal, fail/retry. Same escalating dread as L1's panic sky, but it rewards deliberate investigation rather than speed, which fits the new verb. (Reusing the L1 island corrupted is also cheap to build — it's the existing terrain plus a glitch shader/zone — and emotionally loaded, since it's *your* saved memories under threat.)

**One opinionated design call:** picking the *wrong* checkpoint to rewind to should be a **soft fail**, not a death. The world rewinds but is still broken — and the terminal nudges "you can rewind again." That's the most important real-product message in the whole level: rewind is safe and repeatable, so experimenting costs nothing. A hard fail would teach the opposite.

## Open forks for you

1. **Setting** — I'm recommending the corrupted L1 island (asset reuse + emotional payoff), but a second island or the ship's interior are options if you want each level to feel like new territory.
2. **Pressure** — spreading corruption zone (my rec), a countdown again for consistency with L1, or fully untimed puzzle.
3. **Scope of the search beat** — one disaster with 3 clues to gather (single arc, like above), or 3 smaller corruptions each teaching one command (more L1-like structure, but risks feeling like three tutorials instead of one story).

Also worth flagging from the L1 retro: the y/n checkpoint prompt in L1 is pedagogical scaffolding — the real CLI checkpoints automatically post-commit. Level 2 could include a one-line reveal ("the ship now links memories automatically") to graduate the player off the training wheels without contradicting L1.

Where do you want to push back?

```
136	  const t = clock.elapsedTime;

139

143	  }
144	  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
145	}
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { createTerrain } from "./terrain.js";
3	import { createFirstPerson } from "./firstPerson.js";
4	import { FRAGMENTS, BUILDERS } from "./debris.js";
5	
6	// The walkable island level — LEVEL 1 ("First Memories").
7	//
8	// A Diner-Dash-style loop, one memory at a time, driven by the REAL workflow
9	// typed into the ship's terminal:
10	//   walk up → terminal opens → `git add` (stage) → `git commit` (freeze in ice)
11	//   → Entire offers to link a checkpoint → press y → ship memory restores.
12	// When every memory is linked → run `entire checkpoint list` to review them all.
13	//
14	// PRESSURE: a single level countdown runs the whole time. Bank every memory
15	// before it hits zero, or the un-banked memories melt away and the run fails
16	// (press R to retry). One clock for the whole level — not per-memory.
17	//
18	// Teaching: a commit just freezes the change; linking a CHECKPOINT (the y/n
19	// offer Entire makes after a commit) is what restores it to the ship's memory.
20	
21	const INTERACT_DIST = 8;    // how close (XZ) to open a memory's terminal
22	const TOTAL_TIME = 45;      // seconds to recover & bank EVERY memory (tunable — scary-tight)
23	const MELT_DUR = 1.8;       // seconds the lost memories take to melt away
24	const LOW_TIME = 22;        // clock turns urgent (red, pulsing) under this many seconds
25	const CRIT_TIME = 8;        // clock goes CRITICAL (fast pulse) under this many seconds
26	const PANIC_TIME = 22;      // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red under this many seconds
27	const MEMORY_COUNT = 3;     // calm tutorial: three memories
28	const BEAM_COLOR = 0xffd27a;
29	
30	// Sky panic palette — the whole world reddens as the clock runs out.
31	const SKY_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350);  // lavender dusk (the resting sky/fog)
32	const SKY_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x6e0f16);  // angry crimson
33	const FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);  // deep blood fog closing in
34	const DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);
35	const DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);
36	const SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);  // warm sun
37	const SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);  // hot red alarm light
38	
39	// Where each memory surfaces on the island (world XZ).
40	const PLACEMENTS = [
41	  { x: -34, z: -28 },
42	  { x: 40, z: -18 },
43	  { x: 28, z: 38 },
44	  { x: -30, z: 36 },
45	];
46	
47	// What the terminal asks for at each stage of a memory's recovery.
48	const STEPS = {
49	  dormant:   { type: "command", cmd: "git add",    accept: ["git add"],    hint: "Stage the recovered memory" },
50	  recovered: { type: "command", cmd: "git commit", accept: ["git commit"], hint: "Commit it before the clock runs out" },
51	  frozen:    { type: "confirm", question: "Link this commit to a checkpoint?",
52	               hint: "Entire offers to capture the session behind this commit" },
53	};
54	// After every memory is linked, the player reviews them with this command.
55	const REVIEW_STEP = {
56	  type: "command", cmd: "entire checkpoint list", accept: ["entire checkpoint list"],
57	  hint: "All memories recovered — review what you've banked",
58	};
59	
60	function normalizeCmd(s) {
61	  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
62	}
63	function cmdMatches(input, accepts) {
64	  const n = normalizeCmd(input);
65	  return accepts.some((a) => n === a || n.startsWith(a + " "));
66	}
67	
68	function makeBeamTexture() {
69	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
70	  c.width = 8; c.height = 128;
71	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
72	  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);
73	  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,210,122,0)");
74	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,210,122,0.9)");
75	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
76	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 128);
77	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
78	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
79	  return t;
80	}
81	
82	// Translucent crystal that encases a memory once it's committed.
83	function makeIceBlock() {
84	  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.4, 0);
85	  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
86	    color: 0xbfe9ff,
87	    metalness: 0,
88	    roughness: 0.06,
89	    transmission: 0.5,
90	    thickness: 2.6,
91	    ior: 1.3,
92	    transparent: true,
93	    opacity: 0.62,
94	    emissive: 0x0a2230,
95	    emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
96	    flatShading: true,
97	  });
98	  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
99	}
100	
101	// Floating label above a frozen block — the commit trailer Entire stamps on:
102	//   "Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hash>" (dim key + bright hash).
103	function makeIdSprite(id) {
104	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
105	  c.width = 768; c.height = 64;
106	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
107	  ctx.font = "bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace";
108	  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
109	  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(111,227,255,0.9)";
110	  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
111	
112	  const key = "Entire-Checkpoint: ";
113	  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;
114	  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;
115	  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2;       // center the whole trailer
116	  ctx.textAlign = "left";
117	  ctx.fillStyle = "#8fc4dc";                    // dim trailer key
118	  ctx.fillText(key, x, 34);
119	  ctx.fillStyle = "#dff3ff";                    // bright hash
120	  ctx.fillText(id, x + keyW, 34);
121	
122	  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
123	  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
124	  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
125	    map: tex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
126	  }));
127	  spr.scale.set(30, 2.5, 1);                    // 12:1 canvas → keep text height
128	  return spr;
129	}
130	
131	// Entire checkpoints get a 12-character hex id (e.g. 711044b1fe29).
132	function genCheckpointId() {
133	  const hex = "0123456789abcdef";
134	  let s = "";
135	  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) s += hex[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
136	  return s;
137	}
138	
139	export function createIslandView(renderer, { onExit } = {}) {
140	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
141	
142	  // ---------- scene & sky ----------
143	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
144	  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350); // lavender dusk sky
145	  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0x2a2350, 90, 320);
146	
147	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
148	    62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000
149	  );
150	
151	  // ---------- lighting (soft, warm sun in a lavender sky) ----------
152	  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));
153	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff1dc, 1.5);
154	  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);
155	  scene.add(sun);
156	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));
157	
158	  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
159	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),
160	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x3a3168, side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })
161	  );
162	  scene.add(dome);
163	
164	  // ---------- terrain ----------
165	  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });
166	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
167	
168	  // ---------- memories ----------
169	  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();
170	  const artifacts = [];
171	  FRAGMENTS.slice(0, MEMORY_COUNT).forEach((frag, idx) => {
172	    const place = PLACEMENTS[idx % PLACEMENTS.length];
173	    const groundY = terrain.heightAt(place.x, place.z);
174	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
175	    anchor.position.set(place.x, groundY, place.z);
176	
177	    const model = BUILDERS[frag.kind]();
178	    model.scale.setScalar(14);
179	    model.position.y = 1.4;
180	    anchor.add(model);
181	
182	    // A tall light beam so the memory is findable from across the island.
183	    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
184	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),
185	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
186	        map: beamTex, color: BEAM_COLOR, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5,
187	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
188	        fog: false,
189	      })
190	    );
191	    beam.position.y = 30;
192	    anchor.add(beam);
193	
194	    const glow = new THREE.PointLight(BEAM_COLOR, 8, 40, 2);
195	    glow.position.y = 3;
196	    anchor.add(glow);
197	
198	    scene.add(anchor);
199	    artifacts.push({
200	      anchor, model, beam, glow, fragment: frag,
201	      state: "dormant",   // dormant → recovered (staged) → frozen (committed) → checkpointed
202	      ice: null, idSprite: null, core: null, id: null, iceY: 0,
203	      melting: false, meltT: 0,   // melt-away animation when the level times out
204	      bobOff: Math.random() * 10,
205	    });
206	  });
207	
208	  // ---------- first-person controller ----------
209	  const fp = createFirstPerson(camera, canvas, {
210	    heightAt: terrain.heightAt,
211	    radius: terrain.radius * 0.96,
212	    eyeHeight: 2.6,
213	    speed: 24,
214	  });
215	  scene.add(fp.controls.object);
216	
217	  // ---------- HUD elements ----------
218	  const promptEl = document.getElementById("fp-prompt");
219	  const controlsEl = document.getElementById("fp-controls");
220	  const crosshair = document.getElementById("crosshair");
221	  const islandHud = document.getElementById("island-hud");
222	  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById("tutorial");
223	  const actionBar = document.getElementById("action-bar");
224	  const actionBarFill = document.getElementById("action-bar-fill");
225	  const shipMeter = document.getElementById("ship-meter");
226	  const shipMeterFill = document.getElementById("ship-meter-fill");
227	  const shipMeterPct = document.getElementById("ship-meter-pct");
228	  const ckptCard = document.getElementById("ckpt-card");
229	  const ccId = document.getElementById("cc-id");
230	  const ccSummary = document.getElementById("cc-summary");
231	  const ccAttrFill = document.getElementById("cc-attr-fill");
232	  const ccAttrText = document.getElementById("cc-attr-text");
233	  const termEl = document.getElementById("terminal");
234	  const termHint = document.getElementById("term-hint");
235	  const termInput = document.getElementById("term-input");
236	  const termList = document.getElementById("term-list");
237	  const termMsg = document.getElementById("term-msg");
238	  const termCta = document.getElementById("term-cta");
239	  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("countdown");
240	  const countdownTime = document.getElementById("countdown-time");
241	  const levelFail = document.getElementById("level-fail");
242	  const briefingEl = document.getElementById("briefing");
243	  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById("briefing-start");
244	
245	  let target = null;            // nearest memory in range
246	  let active = false;           // is this view being shown?
247	  let promptText = null;
248	  let controlsLocked = null;
249	  const taught = new Set();
250	  let tutorialTimer = null;
251	  let cardTimer = null;
252	  let msgTimer = null;
253	
254	  // Terminal state
255	  let terminalOpen = false;
256	  let termTarget = null;        // the memory whose terminal is open
257	  let buffer = "";              // what the player has typed
258	  let dismissedMemory = null;   // memory the player Esc'd out of (until they leave)
259	  let reviewMode = false;       // all linked → review with `entire checkpoint list`
260	  let listShown = false;        // the list has been printed
261	
262	  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.
263	  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
264	  let timerRunning = false;
265	  let failed = false;
266	  let started = false;          // briefing dismissed → movement + clock go live
267	
268	  const checkpointedCount = () =>
269	    artifacts.filter((a) => a.state === "checkpointed").length;
270	  const allLinked = () => checkpointedCount() >= artifacts.length;
271	
272	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
273	  function setPrompt(text) {
274	    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;
275	    promptText = text;
276	    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove("hidden"); }
277	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
278	  }
279	  function hideActionBar() { actionBar.classList.add("hidden"); }
280	
281	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
282	  function fmtTime(s) {
283	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
284	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
285	  }
286	  function updateClock() {
287	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
288	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
289	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
290	  }
291	
292	  // How hard the sky is panicking right now: 0 = calm lavender, 1 = full crimson throb.
293	  function panicFactor() {
294	    if (failed) return 1;                         // hold the dread behind the fail screen
295	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
296	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME; // 0 at PANIC_TIME → 1 at 0:00
297	    p *= p;                                        // eased: barely there early, harsh late
298	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {                   // throb once it's critical
299	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
300	      p += 0.14 * throb;
301	    }
302	    return Math.min(1, p);
303	  }
304	  function applyPanicSky() {
305	    const p = panicFactor();
306	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
307	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
308	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
309	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
310	  }
311	  function failLevel() {
312	    if (failed) return;
313	    failed = true;
314	    timerRunning = false;
315	    closeTerminal(false);
316	    fp.detach();                  // freeze the player behind the fail screen
317	    // Un-banked memories always melt; if they ran out the clock on the final
318	    // `entire checkpoint list` (all banked, not yet reviewed), the whole run melts.
319	    for (const a of artifacts) {
320	      if (reviewMode || a.state !== "checkpointed") { a.melting = true; a.meltT = MELT_DUR; }
321	    }
322	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
323	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
324	    levelFail?.classList.remove("hidden");
325	  }
326	  function resetLevel() {
327	    failed = false;
328	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
329	    if (active) fp.attach();      // movement resumes for the new run
330	    for (const a of artifacts) {
331	      if (a.ice) { a.anchor.remove(a.ice); a.ice = null; }
332	      if (a.idSprite) { a.anchor.remove(a.idSprite); a.idSprite = null; }
333	      if (a.core) { a.anchor.remove(a.core); a.core = null; }
334	      a.state = "dormant";
335	      a.id = null;
336	      a.melting = false; a.meltT = 0;
337	      a.model.position.y = 1.4;
338	      setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
339	    }
340	    reviewMode = false;
341	    listShown = false;
342	    dismissedMemory = null;
343	    shipMeter?.classList.remove("is-full");
344	    setPower();
345	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
346	    timerRunning = true;
347	    updateClock();
348	    showTutorial("New attempt — bank all three memories before the clock hits zero.", 4500);
349	  }
350	
351	  // Landing briefing — the level is frozen (no clock, no movement) until START.
352	  function showBriefing() {
353	    timerRunning = false;
354	    fp.detach();
355	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
356	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
357	  }
358	  function startLevel() {
359	    if (started) return;
360	    started = true;
361	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
362	    fp.attach();
363	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
364	    timerRunning = true;
365	    updateClock();
366	    showTutorial("Memories are surfacing — the clock's short. Bank all three before 0:00!", 6500);
367	  }
368	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
369	
370	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
371	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
372	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
373	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
374	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
375	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
376	  }
377	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
378	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
379	    taught.add(key);
380	    showTutorial(text, ms);
381	  }
382	
383	  function setPower() {
384	    const pct = Math.round((checkpointedCount() / artifacts.length) * 100);
385	    // Vertical gauge — fills bottom-up and shifts color as memory is restored.
386	    if (shipMeterFill) shipMeterFill.style.height = pct + "%";
387	    if (shipMeterPct) shipMeterPct.textContent = pct + "%";
388	    if (shipMeter) {
389	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-low", pct > 0 && pct <= 34);
390	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-mid", pct > 34 && pct < 100);
391	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-high", pct >= 100);
392	    }
393	  }
394	  function showCard(a, shipPct) {
395	    if (!ckptCard) return;
396	    ccId.textContent = a.id;
397	    ccSummary.textContent = `Recovered: ${a.fragment.title}`;
398	    ccAttrFill.style.width = shipPct + "%";
399	    ccAttrText.textContent = `ship ${shipPct}% · you ${100 - shipPct}%`;
400	    ckptCard.classList.remove("hidden");
401	    clearTimeout(cardTimer);
402	    cardTimer = setTimeout(() => ckptCard.classList.add("hidden"), 3800);
403	  }
404	
405	  function setControls(locked) {
406	    if (!controlsEl) return;
407	    if (locked === controlsLocked && !controlsEl.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
408	    controlsLocked = locked;
409	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
410	      <span class="control-item">
411	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
412	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
413	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
414	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
415	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
416	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
417	        </span>
418	      </span>
419	      ${locked ? `
420	        <span class="control-item">
421	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
422	          <span class="mouse-hint">
423	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
424	            <span>move mouse</span>
425	          </span>
426	        </span>
427	      ` : ""}
428	      <span class="control-item">
429	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
430	        <span class="key">B</span>
431	      </span>
432	    `;
433	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
434	  }
435	  function hideControls() {
436	    controlsLocked = null;
437	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
438	  }
439	
440	  function setModelOpacity(model, o) {
441	    model.traverse((n) => {
442	      if (n.material) { n.material.transparent = o < 1; n.material.opacity = o; }
443	    });
444	  }
445	
446	  // ---------- terminal ----------
447	  function currentStep() {
448	    if (reviewMode) return REVIEW_STEP;
449	    if (!termTarget) return null;
450	    return STEPS[termTarget.state] || null;
451	  }
452	  function renderTerminal() {
453	    const step = currentStep();
454	    if (!step) return;
455	
456	    if (reviewMode && listShown) {
457	      termHint.textContent = "# entire checkpoint list";
458	      termInput.textContent = "entire checkpoint list";
459	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
460	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = "";
461	      termList?.classList.remove("hidden");
462	      return;
463	    }
464	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
465	
466	    // The hint is just the "why"; the actionable command/keys live in the CTA
467	    // below the input line, where they're the most prominent thing on screen.
468	    termHint.textContent = step.hint ? `# ${step.hint}` : "";
469	    if (step.type === "confirm") {
470	      termInput.textContent = `${step.question}  [y/n]`;
471	      termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
472	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
473	        `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
474	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd><span class="cta-note">link checkpoint</span>` +
475	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-no">N</kbd><span class="cta-note">skip</span>`;
476	    } else {
477	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
478	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
479	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
480	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">${step.cmd}</span>`;
481	    }
482	  }
483	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
484	    if (!termMsg) return;
485	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
486	    termMsg.textContent = text;
487	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
488	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
489	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 2400);
490	  }
491	  function openTerminal(a) {
492	    if (terminalOpen) return;
493	    terminalOpen = true;
494	    termTarget = a;
495	    buffer = "";
496	    fp.detach();                      // freeze movement; keystrokes feed the terminal
497	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
498	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
499	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
500	    renderTerminal();
501	  }
502	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
503	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
504	    if (dismiss && termTarget) dismissedMemory = termTarget;
505	    terminalOpen = false;
506	    termTarget = null;
507	    buffer = "";
508	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
509	    if (active) fp.attach();          // resume walking (addEventListener dedupes)
510	  }
511	  function submitCommand() {
512	    const step = currentStep();
513	    if (!step || step.type !== "command" || listShown) return;
514	    if (cmdMatches(buffer, step.accept)) {
515	      buffer = "";
516	      if (reviewMode) { showCheckpointList(); return; }
517	      runStep(termTarget);            // dormant → stage, recovered → commit
518	      flashTerminal("✓ ok", true);
519	      renderTerminal();
520	    } else {
521	      flashTerminal(`command not recognized — try:  ${step.cmd}`, false);
522	      buffer = "";
523	      renderTerminal();
524	    }
525	  }
526	  function showCheckpointList() {
527	    listShown = true;
528	    timerRunning = false;            // the run is complete — NOW the clock stops
529	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
530	    if (termList) {
531	      termList.innerHTML = artifacts.map((a) =>
532	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id">` +
533	        `<span class="tl-key">Entire-Checkpoint:</span> ${a.id}</span>` +
534	        `<span class="tl-title">recovered: ${a.fragment.title}</span></div>`
535	      ).join("");
536	    }
537	    flashTerminal(`${artifacts.length} checkpoints linked · ship memory restored`, true);
538	    showTutorial("Memory restored — the ship knows the way home. Press Esc to close the terminal, then B to return to orbit.", 0);
539	    renderTerminal();
540	  }
541	
542	  // ---------- state transitions ----------
543	  function runStep(a) {
544	    if (a.state === "dormant") stage(a);
545	    else if (a.state === "recovered") commit(a);
546	  }
547	  function stage(a) {
548	    a.state = "recovered";
549	    teachOnce("staged",
550	      "Recovered and staged. Run `git commit` to freeze it — the clock is ticking.");
551	  }
552	  function commit(a) {
553	    a.state = "frozen";
554	    setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
555	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
556	    ice.position.y = a.model.position.y + 0.6;
557	    a.iceY = ice.position.y;
558	    a.anchor.add(ice);
559	    a.ice = ice;
560	    teachOnce("committed",
561	      "Committed and frozen — locked in. Now Entire offers to link a checkpoint: press y to capture the session behind this commit.");
562	  }
563	  function confirmCheckpoint(yes) {
564	    const a = termTarget;
565	    if (!a || a.state !== "frozen") return;
566	    if (!yes) {
567	      flashTerminal("declined — without a checkpoint the ship can't track this commit. re-approach to link it.", false);
568	      closeTerminal(true);
569	      return;
570	    }
571	    checkpoint(a);
572	    if (allLinked()) {
573	      reviewMode = true;             // clock KEEPS running — the list is the finish line
574	      shipMeter?.classList.add("is-full");
575	      showTutorial("All banked! Now type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00 — hurry!", 0);
576	      renderTerminal();              // stay open → review step
577	    } else {
578	      closeTerminal(false);
579	    }
580	  }
581	  function checkpoint(a) {
582	    a.state = "checkpointed";
583	    a.id = genCheckpointId();
584	    const shipPct = 62 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 23); // 62–84%
585	
586	    if (a.ice) {
587	      a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);
588	      a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
589	      const core = new THREE.PointLight(0x8fe3ff, 6, 26, 2);
590	      core.position.copy(a.ice.position);
591	      a.anchor.add(core);
592	      a.core = core;
593	    }
594	    const spr = makeIdSprite(a.id);
595	    spr.position.y = a.iceY + 5;
596	    a.anchor.add(spr);
597	    a.idSprite = spr;
598	
599	    showCard(a, shipPct);
600	    setPower();
601	    teachOnce("checkpointed",
602	      "Checkpoint linked — Entire stamps it onto the commit via an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer, and the ship remembers a little more.");
603	  }
604	
605	  // ---------- input ----------
606	  function onCanvasClick() {
607	    if (!started) return;                 // briefing up — ignore world clicks
608	    if (active && !fp.isLocked) fp.lock();
609	  }
610	  function onKeyDown(e) {
611	    if (!active) return;
612	
613	    // Landing briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
614	    if (!started) {
615	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
616	      return;
617	    }
618	
619	    // The run failed — only R (retry) does anything.
620	    if (failed) {
621	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
622	      return;
623	    }
624	
625	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
626	    if (terminalOpen) {
627	      if (e.code === "Escape") {
628	        // No bailing on the final review — the clock is still ticking.
629	        if (reviewMode && !listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
630	        closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return;
631	      }
632	      if (reviewMode && listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; } // only Esc closes the list
633	
634	      const step = currentStep();
635	      if (step?.type === "confirm") {
636	        if (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y") { confirmCheckpoint(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
637	        if (e.key === "n" || e.key === "N") { confirmCheckpoint(false); e.preventDefault(); return; }
638	        e.preventDefault();
639	        return;
640	      }
641	      // command mode
642	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
643	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
644	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
645	        buffer += e.key;
646	        renderTerminal();
647	        e.preventDefault();
648	      }
649	      return;
650	    }
651	
652	    // Walking around (terminal closed).
653	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
654	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
655	  }
656	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
657	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
658	
659	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
660	  function refreshHud() {
661	    if (!started || terminalOpen) {     // briefing up or terminal open → no walk HUD
662	      setPrompt(null);
663	      hideControls();
664	      hideActionBar();
665	      return;
666	    }
667	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
668	    hideActionBar();
669	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
670	    if (listShown) { setPrompt("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit"); return; }
671	    setPrompt("Find the surfacing memory");
672	  }
673	
674	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
675	  function update(dt, t) {
676	    fp.update(dt);
677	
678	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
679	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed) {
680	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
681	      updateClock();
682	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
683	    }
684	    applyPanicSky();   // the sky reacts to the clock every frame
685	
686	    // Nearest memory within reach (ignoring height).
687	    let nearest = null, nd = Infinity;
688	    for (const a of artifacts) {
689	      const dx = camera.position.x - a.anchor.position.x;
690	      const dz = camera.position.z - a.anchor.position.z;
691	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
692	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; nearest = a; }
693	    }
694	    target = nd <= INTERACT_DIST ? nearest : null;
695	
696	    // Auto open/close the ship's terminal as you reach a memory.
697	    // (In review mode the terminal is driven manually, so leave it alone.)
698	    if (active && started && !reviewMode && !failed) {
699	      if (dismissedMemory && target !== dismissedMemory) dismissedMemory = null;
700	      const canOpen = target && target.state !== "checkpointed" && target !== dismissedMemory;
701	      if (canOpen && !terminalOpen) openTerminal(target);
702	      else if (!canOpen && terminalOpen) closeTerminal(false);
703	    }
704	
705	    for (const a of artifacts) {
706	      // Melting away after a time-out — sink and fade, then disappear.
707	      if (a.melting) {
708	        a.meltT = Math.max(0, a.meltT - dt);
709	        const k = a.meltT / MELT_DUR;
710	        setModelOpacity(a.model, k);
711	        a.model.position.y -= dt * 2.4;
712	        if (a.ice) a.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * k;
713	        a.beam.visible = false;
714	        a.glow.visible = false;
715	        continue;
716	      }
717	
718	      const encased = a.state === "frozen" || a.state === "checkpointed";
719	
720	      // Idle spin/bob until the memory is encased in ice.
721	      if (!encased) {
722	        a.model.rotation.y += dt * 0.6;
723	        a.model.position.y = 1.4 + Math.sin((t + a.bobOff) * 1.5) * 0.4;
724	        setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
725	      }
726	
727	      // Beam + glow: bright while findable, gone once frozen.
728	      const lit = a.state === "dormant" || a.state === "recovered";
729	      let o = 0;
730	      if (a.state === "dormant") o = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.12;
731	      else if (a.state === "recovered") o = 0.22;
732	      a.beam.material.opacity = o;
733	      a.beam.visible = o > 0.01;
734	      a.glow.visible = lit;
735	    }
736	
737	    if (terminalOpen) renderTerminal(); // reflect fade-driven state changes
738	    if (active) refreshHud();
739	  }
740	
741	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
742	  function enter() {
743	    active = true;
744	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
745	    camera.lookAt(0, terrain.heightAt(0, 0) + 6, 0);
746	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
747	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
748	    islandHud?.classList.remove("hidden");
749	    setControls(false);
750	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
751	    setPower();
752	    if (!started) {                 // fresh landing — read the briefing, then START
753	      showBriefing();
754	    } else if (listShown) {         // truly done — the list has been run
755	      fp.attach();
756	      timerRunning = false;
757	      showTutorial("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit.", 0);
758	    } else if (failed) {
759	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
760	    } else {
761	      fp.attach();
762	      timerRunning = true;          // resume the level clock
763	      if (reviewMode) {             // resume the timed final review
764	        openTerminal(artifacts[0]); // termTarget unused in review; reopens the prompt
765	        showTutorial("Type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00!", 0);
766	      } else {
767	        showTutorial("Memories are surfacing — the clock's short. Bank all three before 0:00!", 6500);
768	      }
769	    }
770	    updateClock();
771	    applyPanicSky();   // start on the right sky (calm unless we resumed low on time)
772	  }
773	  function exit() {
774	    active = false;
775	    timerRunning = false;           // pause the clock while in orbit
776	    closeTerminal(false);
777	    fp.unlock();
778	    fp.detach();
779	    canvas.removeEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
780	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
781	    setPrompt(null);
782	    hideControls();
783	    hideActionBar();
784	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
785	    ckptCard?.classList.add("hidden");
786	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
787	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
788	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
789	    islandHud?.classList.add("hidden");
790	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
791	  }
792	  function resize() {
793	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
794	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
795	  }
796	
797	  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };
798	}
799	
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { makeNoise3D, makeFbm } from "./noise.js";
3	
4	// A walkable island: a displaced plane with a radial falloff so the land rises
5	// out of a lavender sea and tapers back into it at the edges. Palette matches
6	// the planet — gold highlands, pale-gold shore, lavender shallows.
7	
8	// Colors as 0..1 rgb triples (vertex colors are linear-ish; kept soft).
9	const C_DEEP = [0.27, 0.21, 0.46];    // lavender lowland near water
10	const C_SHORE = [0.78, 0.66, 0.42];   // pale gold sand
11	const C_GOLD = [0.86, 0.66, 0.27];    // gold midland
12	const C_PEAK = [0.96, 0.86, 0.62];    // bright gold / cream highland
13	
14	function mix(a, b, t) {
15	  return [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t];
16	}
17	function ramp(t) {
18	  if (t < 0.18) return mix(C_DEEP, C_SHORE, t / 0.18);
19	  if (t < 0.5) return mix(C_SHORE, C_GOLD, (t - 0.18) / 0.32);
20	  return mix(C_GOLD, C_PEAK, Math.min(1, (t - 0.5) / 0.5));
21	}
22	
23	/**
24	 * Build the island terrain.
25	 *
26	 * @param {object} opts
27	 * @param {number} opts.size     world units across (square)
28	 * @param {number} opts.segments grid resolution
29	 * @param {number} opts.maxHeight peak elevation in world units
30	 * @param {number} opts.seed
31	 * @returns {{ mesh, water, size, maxHeight, heightAt(x,z), radius }}
32	 */
33	export function createTerrain({
34	  size = 200,
35	  segments = 220,
36	  maxHeight = 26,
37	  seed = 1337,
38	} = {}) {
39	  const noise = makeNoise3D(seed);
40	  const fbm = makeFbm(noise, { octaves: 5, lacunarity: 2.1, gain: 0.5 });
41	
42	  const half = size / 2;
43	  const seaLevel = 0; // world-space y of the surrounding water plane
44	
45	  // Raw elevation field in world units for a given world (x,z). Combines fbm
46	  // detail with a smooth radial island mask (1 at centre → 0 past the radius).
47	  const islandRadius = half * 0.86;
48	  function rawHeight(x, z) {
49	    const d = Math.hypot(x, z) / islandRadius;
50	    // Smooth falloff: flat-ish plateau in the middle, sinks below sea at edge.
51	    const mask = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1 - d * d, 0, 1);
52	    const n = fbm(x * 0.015, 0, z * 0.015) * 0.5 + 0.5; // 0..1, gentle hills
53	    const ridges = fbm(x * 0.05 + 100, 5, z * 0.05 - 100) * 0.5 + 0.5; // finer
54	    const e = (n * 0.7 + ridges * 0.3) * mask;
55	    // Push the shoreline down so the centre clearly stands above the water.
56	    return (e - 0.18) * maxHeight;
57	  }
58	
59	  // Bake the grid once into a Float32 height table for fast bilinear queries.
60	  const n = segments + 1;
61	  const step = size / segments;
62	  const table = new Float32Array(n * n);
63	  for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
64	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
65	      const x = -half + i * step;
66	      const z = -half + j * step;
67	      table[j * n + i] = rawHeight(x, z);
68	    }
69	  }
70	
71	  // Bilinear height lookup in world space, clamped to the grid.
72	  function heightAt(x, z) {
73	    const fx = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((x + half) / step, 0, segments);
74	    const fz = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((z + half) / step, 0, segments);
75	    const i0 = Math.floor(fx), j0 = Math.floor(fz);
76	    const i1 = Math.min(i0 + 1, segments), j1 = Math.min(j0 + 1, segments);
77	    const tx = fx - i0, tz = fz - j0;
78	    const h00 = table[j0 * n + i0], h10 = table[j0 * n + i1];
79	    const h01 = table[j1 * n + i0], h11 = table[j1 * n + i1];
80	    const a = h00 + (h10 - h00) * tx;
81	    const b = h01 + (h11 - h01) * tx;
82	    return a + (b - a) * tz;
83	  }
84	
85	  // ---- mesh ----
86	  const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size, size, segments, segments);
87	  geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); // lay flat on the XZ plane, +Y up
88	  const pos = geo.attributes.position;
89	  const colors = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);
90	  for (let v = 0; v < pos.count; v++) {
91	    const x = pos.getX(v);
92	    const z = pos.getZ(v);
93	    const y = table[
94	      Math.round((z + half) / step) * n + Math.round((x + half) / step)
95	    ];
96	    pos.setY(v, y);
97	    // Color by height above sea; underwater fringe stays lavender.
98	    const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(y / maxHeight + 0.18, 0, 1);
99	    const c = ramp(t);
100	    colors[v * 3] = c[0]; colors[v * 3 + 1] = c[1]; colors[v * 3 + 2] = c[2];
101	  }
102	  geo.setAttribute("color", new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
103	  geo.computeVertexNormals();
104	
105	  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
106	    geo,
107	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
108	      vertexColors: true,
109	      roughness: 0.85,
110	      metalness: 0.15, // a faint sheen so it still feels related to the gold world
111	      flatShading: false,
112	    })
113	  );
114	  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
115	
116	  // ---- surrounding water ----
117	  const water = new THREE.Mesh(
118	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size * 4, size * 4),
119	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
120	      color: 0x4a3a86,        // lavender sea, matching the planet's DEEP tone
121	      transparent: true,
122	      opacity: 0.86,
123	      roughness: 0.25,
124	      metalness: 0.0,
125	    })
126	  );
127	  water.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
128	  water.position.y = seaLevel;
129	
130	  return { mesh, water, size, maxHeight, heightAt, radius: islandRadius, seaLevel };
131	}
132
```

```
3	// Radial-gradient sprite used as the attention halo under each debris item.
4	function makeHaloTexture(color = "#6fe3ff") {
5	  const s = 128;
6	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
7	  c.width = c.height = s;
8	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
9	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(s / 2, s / 2, 0, s / 2, s / 2, s / 2);
10	  g.addColorStop(0, color);
11	  g.addColorStop(0.25, color);
12	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
13	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
14	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, s, s);
15	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
16	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;

136	// Convert lat/lon (degrees) to a point on a sphere of the given radius.
137	function latLonToVec3(lat, lon, radius) {
138	  const phi = (90 - lat) * (Math.PI / 180);
139	  const theta = (lon + 180) * (Math.PI / 180);
140	  return new THREE.Vector3(
141	    -radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
142	    radius * Math.cos(phi),
143	    radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta)
144	  );
145	}
146	
147	/**
148	 * Build debris anchors on the planet surface. Returns the group (to add to the
149	 * planet so they rotate together) and the list of pickable meshes for
150	 * raycasting, each carrying its fragment data in userData.
151	 */
152	export function createDebris(planetRadius) {
153	  const group = new THREE.Group();
154	  const pickables = [];
155	
156	  for (const frag of FRAGMENTS) {
157	    const pos = latLonToVec3(frag.lat, frag.lon, planetRadius);
158	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
159	    anchor.position.copy(pos);
160	    // Orient so local +Y points away from the planet centre.
161	    anchor.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(
162	      new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
163	      pos.clone().normalize()
164	    );
165	
166	    const model = BUILDERS[frag.kind]();
167	    model.position.y = 0.02;
168	
169	    // Halo to make it findable; doubles as the click target (big & flat).
170	    const halo = new THREE.Mesh(
171	      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.34, 0.34),
172	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
173	        map: haloTexture(),
174	        transparent: true,
175	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
176	        depthWrite: false,
177	        opacity: 0.9,
178	      })
179	    );
180	    halo.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
181	    halo.position.y = 0.005;
182	
183	    anchor.add(halo, model);
184	    anchor.userData = { fragment: frag, halo, model, baseScale: 1 };
185	    group.add(anchor);
186	    pickables.push(anchor);
187	  }
188	
189	  return { group, pickables };
190	}
191
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { PointerLockControls } from "three/addons/controls/PointerLockControls.js";
3	
4	// First-person walker: PointerLockControls for mouse-look + keyboard movement
5	// that hugs the terrain. Click the canvas to look around; Escape releases the mouse.
6	
7	export function createFirstPerson(camera, domElement, {
8	  heightAt,
9	  radius = 80,        // keep the walker on the island, not out at sea
10	  eyeHeight = 2.4,
11	  speed = 22,         // world units / second
12	} = {}) {
13	  const controls = new PointerLockControls(camera, domElement);
14	
15	  const keys = { f: false, b: false, l: false, r: false };
16	  function onKey(down) {
17	    return (e) => {
18	      switch (e.code) {
19	        case "KeyW": case "ArrowUp": keys.f = down; break;
20	        case "KeyS": case "ArrowDown": keys.b = down; break;
21	        case "KeyA": case "ArrowLeft": keys.l = down; break;
22	        case "KeyD": case "ArrowRight": keys.r = down; break;
23	        default: return;
24	      }
25	      // Don't let arrow keys scroll the page while walking.
26	      if (down) e.preventDefault();
27	    };
28	  }
29	  const onKeyDown = onKey(true);
30	  const onKeyUp = onKey(false);
31	
32	  // Place the walker on the ground at a given (x, z).
33	  function groundAt(x, z) {
34	    camera.position.set(x, heightAt(x, z) + eyeHeight, z);
35	  }
36	
37	  function update(dt) {
38	    if (controls.isLocked) {
39	      const d = speed * dt;
40	      // Forward/back and strafe are horizontal; height is re-applied below.
41	      if (keys.f) controls.moveForward(d);
42	      if (keys.b) controls.moveForward(-d);
43	      if (keys.r) controls.moveRight(d);
44	      if (keys.l) controls.moveRight(-d);
45	
46	      // Clamp to the island so you can't walk off into the sea.
47	      const p = camera.position;
48	      const dist = Math.hypot(p.x, p.z);
49	      if (dist > radius) {
50	        const s = radius / dist;
51	        p.x *= s; p.z *= s;
52	      }
53	    }
54	    // Always glue the eye to the terrain (even while standing still after a
55	    // teleport/reset), so the ground never clips through the camera.
56	    camera.position.y = heightAt(camera.position.x, camera.position.z) + eyeHeight;
57	  }
58	
59	  function attach() {
60	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
61	    window.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
62	  }
63	  function detach() {
64	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
65	    window.removeEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
66	    keys.f = keys.b = keys.l = keys.r = false;
67	  }
68	
69	  return {
70	    controls,
71	    update,
72	    groundAt,
73	    attach,
74	    detach,
75	    lock: () => controls.lock(),
76	    unlock: () => controls.unlock(),
77	    get isLocked() { return controls.isLocked; },
78	  };
79	}
80
```

```
8	const PLANET_RADIUS = 2;
9	const PLANET_SEED = 42;
10	const ISLAND_ANGLE = 0.32;      // angular radius of the landing region (radians)
11	
12	// Bird's-eye view: orbit the planet and click the pinned island to land on it.
13	export function createPlanetView(renderer, { onIslandClick } = {}) {
14	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
15	
16	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
17	  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x07050f); // soft deep-violet void
18	
19	  // Image-based lighting so the gold islands have something to reflect.
20	  const pmrem = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
21	  scene.environment = pmrem.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;
22	
23	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
24	    45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000
25	  );
26	  camera.position.set(0, 1.4, 6.5);
27	
28	  const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, canvas);
29	  controls.enableDamping = true;
30	  controls.dampingFactor = 0.06;
31	  controls.enablePan = false;
32	  controls.minDistance = 3.0;
33	  controls.maxDistance = 9.0;
34	  controls.rotateSpeed = 0.5;
35	  controls.autoRotate = false;
36	
37	  // ---------- lighting ----------
38	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff4e8, 1.8);
39	  sun.position.set(5, 2.5, 4);
40	  scene.add(sun);
41	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.25));
42	  const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x8a7ab8, 0.6);
43	  rim.position.set(-5, -1, -4);
44	  scene.add(rim);
45	
46	  // ---------- starfield ----------
47	  scene.add(makeStars());
48	
49	  // ---------- planet ----------
50	  const planetGroup = new THREE.Group();
51	  scene.add(planetGroup);
52	  planetGroup.rotation.z = 0.42; // Saturn-like axial tilt so the ring reads
53	
54	  const { colorMap, bumpMap, roughnessMap, metalnessMap, cloudMap } =
55	    generatePlanetTextures({ seed: PLANET_SEED });
56	
57	  const planet = new THREE.Mesh(
58	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(PLANET_RADIUS, 128, 128),
59	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
60	      map: colorMap,
61	      bumpMap,
62	      bumpScale: 0.015,
63	      roughnessMap,
64	      roughness: 1.0,
65	      metalnessMap,
66	      metalness: 1.0,
67	      envMapIntensity: 0.3,
68	    })
69	  );
70	  planetGroup.add(planet);
71	
72	  const clouds = new THREE.Mesh(
73	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(PLANET_RADIUS * 1.012, 96, 96),
74	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
75	      map: cloudMap, transparent: true, depthWrite: false,
76	      color: 0xf2ecff, opacity: 0.4, roughness: 1.0,
77	    })
78	  );
79	  planetGroup.add(clouds);
80	
81	  planetGroup.add(makeAtmosphere(PLANET_RADIUS * 1.09, {
82	    color: new THREE.Color(0xc9a6ff), power: 2.2, intensity: 0.9,
83	  }));
84	
85	  planetGroup.add(makeRing(PLANET_RADIUS));
86	
87	  // ---------- the landable island ----------
88	  // Find a guaranteed patch of gold land, then anchor a marker on the texel
89	  // that paints it (geometry UV convention) so the pin sits on visible land.
90	  const sampler = createElevationSampler({ seed: PLANET_SEED });
91	  const spot = sampler.findLandSpots({ latLimit: 55, step: 3 })[0]
92	    || { lat: 20, lon: 40 };
93	  const islandLocalPos = latLonToSurface(spot.lat, spot.lon, PLANET_RADIUS);
94	  const islandLocalDir = islandLocalPos.clone().normalize();
95	
96	  // An invisible anchor on the surface we can read a world position from, and
97	  // a thin outline ring (NOT a glow) that fades in only on hover.
98	  const anchor = new THREE.Object3D();
99	  anchor.position.copy(islandLocalPos);
100	  anchor.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), islandLocalDir);
101	  planet.add(anchor); // rides the planet so it tracks rotation
102	
103	  const ringRadius = PLANET_RADIUS * Math.sin(ISLAND_ANGLE);
104	  const ringDepth = PLANET_RADIUS * (1 - Math.cos(ISLAND_ANGLE));
105	  const outline = new THREE.Mesh(
106	    new THREE.RingGeometry(ringRadius * 0.94, ringRadius, 64),
107	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
108	      color: 0xfff4e8, transparent: true, opacity: 0,
109	      side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false,
110	    })
111	  );
112	  outline.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;       // lie flat on the surface
113	  outline.position.y = -ringDepth;          // drop to the sphere rim
114	  outline.renderOrder = 5;
115	  anchor.add(outline);
116	
117	  // ---------- HTML pin ----------
118	  const pin = document.getElementById("island-pin");
119	  const pinClick = () => { if (active) onIslandClick?.(); };
120	  const pinEnter = () => { hoverPin = true; };
121	  const pinLeave = () => { hoverPin = false; };
122	
123	  // ---------- interaction (only while this view is active) ----------
124	  const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
125	  const pointer = new THREE.Vector2();
126	  const tmpWorld = new THREE.Vector3();
127	  const tmpLocal = new THREE.Vector3();
128	  let hoverRegion = false; // pointer is over the island on the globe
129	  let hoverPin = false;    // pointer is over the HTML pin
130	  let islandFaces = false; // island is on the near side, facing camera
131	  let pointerDownAt = null;
132	  let active = false;
133	
134	  // Is a screen point over the island region of the globe?
135	  function overIsland(event) {
136	    if (!islandFaces) return false;
137	    pointer.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
138	    pointer.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;
139	    raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);
140	    const hit = raycaster.intersectObject(planet, false)[0];
141	    if (!hit) return false;
142	    // Compare the hit direction (in planet-local space) to the island centre.
143	    tmpLocal.copy(hit.point);
144	    planet.worldToLocal(tmpLocal).normalize();
145	    return tmpLocal.angleTo(islandLocalDir) < ISLAND_ANGLE;
146	  }
147	
148	  function onPointerMove(e) {
149	    const over = overIsland(e);
150	    if (over !== hoverRegion) {
151	      hoverRegion = over;
152	      canvas.style.cursor = over ? "pointer" : "default";
153	    }
154	  }
155	  function onPointerDown(e) { pointerDownAt = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }; }
156	  function onPointerUp(e) {
157	    if (!pointerDownAt) return;
158	    const moved = Math.hypot(e.clientX - pointerDownAt.x, e.clientY - pointerDownAt.y);
159	    pointerDownAt = null;
160	    if (moved > 6) return; // was an orbit drag
161	    if (overIsland(e)) onIslandClick?.();
162	  }
163	
164	  function update(dt, t) {
165	    planet.rotation.y += dt * 0.02; // gentle drift
166	    clouds.rotation.y += dt * 0.03;
167	    controls.update();
168	
169	    // Track the island in screen space for the pin, and tell if it faces us.
170	    anchor.getWorldPosition(tmpWorld);
171	    const camDir = camera.position.clone().normalize();
172	    islandFaces = tmpWorld.clone().normalize().dot(camDir) > 0.25;
173	
174	    const hot = (hoverRegion || hoverPin) && islandFaces;
175	    outline.material.opacity = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
176	      outline.material.opacity, hot ? 0.95 : 0, 0.2
177	    );
178	
179	    if (active && pin) {
180	      if (islandFaces) {
181	        tmpWorld.project(camera);
182	        const x = (tmpWorld.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * window.innerWidth;
183	        const y = (-tmpWorld.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * window.innerHeight;
184	        pin.style.left = `${x}px`;
185	        pin.style.top = `${y}px`;
186	        pin.classList.remove("hidden");
187	        pin.classList.toggle("is-hot", hoverRegion);
188	      } else {
189	        pin.classList.add("hidden");
190	      }
191	    }
192	  }
193	
194	  function enter() {
195	    active = true;
196	    // Frame the island so it's front-and-centre the moment you land here.
197	    scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
198	    anchor.getWorldPosition(tmpWorld);
199	    const dir = tmpWorld.clone().normalize();
200	    camera.position.copy(dir).multiplyScalar(6.4);
201	    camera.position.y += 0.7; // tip the island slightly below centre
202	    controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
203	    controls.update();
204	
205	    canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", onPointerMove);
206	    canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
207	    canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", onPointerUp);
208	    pin?.addEventListener("click", pinClick);
209	    pin?.addEventListener("pointerenter", pinEnter);
210	    pin?.addEventListener("pointerleave", pinLeave);
211	  }
212	  function exit() {
213	    active = false;
214	    hoverRegion = hoverPin = false;
215	    canvas.style.cursor = "default";
216	    canvas.removeEventListener("pointermove", onPointerMove);
217	    canvas.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
218	    canvas.removeEventListener("pointerup", onPointerUp);
219	    pin?.removeEventListener("click", pinClick);
220	    pin?.removeEventListener("pointerenter", pinEnter);
221	    pin?.removeEventListener("pointerleave", pinLeave);
222	    pin?.classList.add("hidden");
223	  }
224	  function resize() {
225	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
226	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
227	  }
228	
229	  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };
230	}
231	
232	// ---- helpers ----
233	
234	function makeStars() {
235	  const count = 2400;
236	  const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
237	  const colors = new Float32Array(count * 3);
238	  const tint = new THREE.Color();
239	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
240	    const r = 200 + Math.random() * 200;
241	    const u = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
242	    const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
243	    const s = Math.sqrt(1 - u * u);
244	    positions[i * 3] = r * s * Math.cos(a);
245	    positions[i * 3 + 1] = r * u;
246	    positions[i * 3 + 2] = r * s * Math.sin(a);
247	    const warm = Math.random();
248	    tint.setHSL(0.62 + warm * 0.18, 0.35, 0.62 + Math.random() * 0.3);
249	    colors[i * 3] = tint.r; colors[i * 3 + 1] = tint.g; colors[i * 3 + 2] = tint.b;
250	  }
251	  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
252	  geo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
253	  geo.setAttribute("color", new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
254	  const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
255	    size: 1.1, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
256	    transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false,
257	  });
258	  return new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
259	}
260	
261	// Convert a texture (lat, lon) to the matching point on the SphereGeometry,
262	// using three's UV → vertex convention so the marker sits on the painted land.
263	function latLonToSurface(latDeg, lonDeg, radius) {
264	  const u = (lonDeg + 180) / 360;
265	  const v = (90 - latDeg) / 180;
266	  const theta = u * Math.PI * 2;
267	  const phi = v * Math.PI;
268	  return new THREE.Vector3(
269	    -radius * Math.cos(theta) * Math.sin(phi),
270	    radius * Math.cos(phi),
271	    radius * Math.sin(theta) * Math.sin(phi)
272	  );
273	}
274
```
