334	
335	.bf-card {
336	  width: min(540px, 92vw);
337	  max-height: 88vh;
338	  overflow-y: auto;
339	  padding: 30px 34px 26px;
340	  text-align: left;
341	  background: var(--panel-bg);
342	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.28);
343	  border-radius: 16px;
344	  box-shadow: 0 26px 80px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.7), 0 0 50px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);
345	  animation: bf-rise 0.55s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) both;
346	}
347	
348	@keyframes bf-rise {
349	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(14px) scale(0.98); }
350	  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
351	}
352	
353	.bf-tag {
354	  font-size: 11px;
355	  letter-spacing: 0.26em;
356	  color: var(--accent);
357	  opacity: 0.85;
358	  margin-bottom: 8px;
359	}
360	.bf-title {
361	  font-size: 28px;
362	  font-weight: 700;
363	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
364	  color: var(--ink);
365	  margin-bottom: 14px;
366	}
367	.bf-story {
368	  font-size: 13.5px;
369	  line-height: 1.65;
370	  color: var(--ink);
371	  opacity: 0.82;
372	  margin-bottom: 20px;
373	}
374	.bf-mission {
375	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
376	  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
377	  padding: 16px 0;
378	  margin-bottom: 18px;
379	}
380	.bf-mission-head {
381	  font-size: 11px;
382	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
383	  color: var(--accent-warm);
384	  margin-bottom: 12px;
385	}
386	.bf-steps {
387	  list-style: none;
388	  counter-reset: bf;
389	  display: grid;
390	  gap: 11px;
391	}
392	.bf-steps li {
393	  counter-increment: bf;
394	  position: relative;
395	  padding-left: 34px;
396	  font-size: 13px;
397	  line-height: 1.5;
398	  color: var(--ink);
399	  opacity: 0.9;
400	}
401	.bf-steps li::before {
402	  content: counter(bf);
403	  position: absolute;
404	  left: 0;
405	  top: -1px;
406	  width: 22px;
407	  height: 22px;
408	  display: grid;
409	  place-items: center;
410	  font-size: 11.5px;
411	  font-weight: 700;
412	  color: var(--accent);
413	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.4);
414	  border-radius: 50%;
415	}
416	.bf-steps code {
417	  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;
418	  font-size: 12px;
419	  padding: 1px 6px;
420	  border-radius: 4px;
421	  background: rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.1);
422	  color: var(--accent);
423	}
424	.bf-steps strong { color: var(--ink); font-weight: 700; }
425	.bf-controls {
426	  margin-bottom: 18px;
427	}
428	.bf-ctrl-grid {
429	  display: grid;
430	  gap: 10px;
431	}
432	.bf-ctrl {
433	  display: flex;
434	  align-items: center;
435	  gap: 8px;
436	  font-size: 12.5px;
437	  color: var(--ink);
438	  opacity: 0.9;
439	}
440	.bf-keys {
441	  display: inline-flex;
442	  gap: 3px;
443	}
444	.bf-keys .bf-key { margin: 0; }
445	.bf-dim { opacity: 0.55; }
446	
447	.bf-warn {
448	  font-size: 12.5px;
449	  line-height: 1.5;
450	  color: #ff8a78;
451	  opacity: 0.92;
452	  margin-bottom: 22px;
453	}
454	#briefing-start {
455	  display: block;
456	  width: 100%;
457	  padding: 13px 20px;
458	  font-family: inherit;
459	  font-size: 14px;
460	  font-weight: 700;
461	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
462	  color: #04121a;
463	  cursor: pointer;
464	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3aa6c8, var(--accent));
465	  border: none;
466	  border-radius: 10px;
467	  box-shadow: 0 0 26px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.3);
468	  transition: transform 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
469	}
470	#briefing-start:hover {
471	  transform: translateY(-1px);
472	  box-shadow: 0 0 36px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.5);
473	}
474	#briefing-start:active { transform: translateY(0); }
475	.bf-hint {
476	  margin-top: 12px;
477	  text-align: center;
478	  font-size: 12px;
479	  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
480	  color: var(--ink);
481	  opacity: 0.6;
482	}
483	.bf-key {
484	  display: inline-block;
485	  padding: 2px 7px;
486	  margin: 0 2px;
487	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.45);
488	  border-radius: 4px;
489	  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;
490	  font-weight: 600;
491	  color: var(--ink);
492	}
493	
494	#level-fail {
495	  position: absolute;
496	  top: 50%;
497	  left: 50%;
498	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
499	  width: min(380px, 86vw);
500	  padding: 26px 28px;
501	  text-align: center;
502	  background: rgba(20, 8, 12, 0.9);
503	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 90, 90, 0.5);
504	  border-radius: 12px;
505	  box-shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.6), 0 0 36px rgba(255, 90, 90, 0.2);
506	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
507	}
508	
509	.lf-title {
510	  font-size: 20px;
511	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
512	  font-weight: 700;
513	  color: #ff8a78;
514	  margin-bottom: 10px;
515	}
516	.lf-sub {
517	  font-size: 13px;
518	  line-height: 1.5;
519	  color: var(--ink);
520	  opacity: 0.85;
521	  margin-bottom: 18px;
522	}
523	.lf-hint {
524	  font-size: 12.5px;
525	  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
526	  color: var(--ink);
527	  opacity: 0.75;
528	}
529	.lf-key {
530	  display: inline-block;
531	  min-width: 20px;
532	  padding: 2px 7px;
533	  margin: 0 2px;
534	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.45);
535	  border-radius: 4px;
536	  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;
537	  font-weight: 600;
538	  color: var(--ink);
539	}
540	
541	/* Teaching line */
542	#tutorial {
543	  position: absolute;
544	  top: 132px;
545	  left: 50%;
546	  transform: translateX(-50%);
547	  max-width: min(560px, calc(100vw - 48px));
548	  padding: 10px 18px;
549	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.62);
550	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.32);
551	  border-radius: 6px;
552	  font-size: 12.5px;
553	  line-height: 1.5;
554	  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
555	  text-align: center;
556	  color: var(--ink);
557	  opacity: 0;
558	  transition: opacity 0.4s ease;
559	}
560	#tutorial:not(.hidden) { opacity: 1; }
561	
562	/* Action progress bar (recover fill / fade countdown) */
563	#action-bar {
564	  position: absolute;
565	  bottom: 176px;
566	  left: 50%;
567	  transform: translateX(-50%);
568	  width: 220px;
569	  height: 6px;
570	  border-radius: 4px;
571	  background: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.12);
572	  overflow: hidden;
573	}
574	
575	#action-bar-fill {
576	  display: block;
577	  height: 100%;
578	  width: 0%;
579	  border-radius: 4px;
580	}
581	
582	#action-bar.is-recover #action-bar-fill {
583	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3aa6c8, var(--accent));
584	  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.6);
585	}
586	
587	#action-bar.is-fade #action-bar-fill {
588	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ff7a5c, var(--accent-warm));
589	  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.6);
590	}
591	
592	/* Checkpoint record card (the middle-ground "anatomy") */
593	#ckpt-card {
594	  position: absolute;
595	  top: 50%;
596	  left: 50%;
597	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.96);
598	  width: min(340px, 86vw);
599	  padding: 18px 20px;
600	  background: rgba(8, 16, 28, 0.9);
601	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.4);
602	  border-radius: 10px;
603	  box-shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.6), 0 0 30px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.18);
604	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
605	  opacity: 0;
606	  transition: opacity 0.35s ease, transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
607	}
608	
609	#ckpt-card:not(.hidden) {
610	  opacity: 1;
611	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1);
612	}
613	
614	.cc-head {
615	  font-size: 11px;
616	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
617	  color: var(--accent);
618	}
619	#cc-id { color: var(--ink); }
620	
621	.cc-summary {
622	  margin: 12px 0 14px;
623	  font-size: 15px;
624	  line-height: 1.4;
625	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
626	}
627	
628	.cc-attr {
629	  display: flex;
630	  align-items: center;
631	  gap: 10px;
632	}
633	
634	.cc-attr-bar {
635	  position: relative;
636	  flex: 1;
637	  height: 6px;
638	  border-radius: 4px;
639	  background: rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.22);
640	  overflow: hidden;
641	}
642	
643	#cc-attr-fill {
644	  position: absolute;
645	  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
646	  width: 70%;
647	  background: var(--accent);
648	}
649	
650	#cc-attr-text {
651	  font-size: 10.5px;
652	  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
653	  opacity: 0.75;
654	  white-space: nowrap;
655	}
656	
657	.cc-trailer {
658	  margin-top: 13px;
659	  padding-top: 11px;
660	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
661	  font-size: 11px;
662	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
663	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.5);
664	}
665	.cc-trailer #cc-id { color: var(--accent); opacity: 1; }
666	
667	.cc-foot {
668	  margin-top: 10px;
669	  font-size: 10px;
670	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
671	  text-transform: uppercase;
672	  color: var(--accent);
673	  opacity: 0.75;
674	}
675	
676	/* Ship terminal — type the real commands */
677	#terminal {
678	  position: absolute;
679	  bottom: 70px;
680	  left: 50%;
681	  transform: translateX(-50%);
682	  width: min(560px, calc(100vw - 40px));
683	  padding: 14px 16px;
684	  background: rgba(4, 10, 18, 0.88);
685	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.42);
686	  border-radius: 8px;
687	  box-shadow: 0 14px 40px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.55), 0 0 24px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);
688	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
689	  text-align: left;
690	}
691	
692	#term-hint {
693	  font-size: 12.5px;
694	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
695	  color: var(--ink);
696	  opacity: 0.68;
697	  margin-bottom: 10px;
698	}
699	
700	#term-line {
701	  display: flex;
702	  align-items: center;
703	  font-size: 16px;
704	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
705	}
706	
707	.term-prompt {
708	  color: var(--accent);
709	  opacity: 0.9;
710	  margin-right: 8px;
711	}
712	
713	#term-input { color: var(--ink); white-space: pre; }
714	#term-input.is-dim { color: var(--accent-warm); opacity: 0.92; }
715	
716	/* Call-to-action: the actionable command/keys — the brightest thing here */
717	#term-cta {
718	  display: flex;
719	  align-items: center;
720	  gap: 10px;
721	  margin-top: 13px;
722	}
723	#term-cta:empty { display: none; }
724	.cta-label {
725	  font-size: 10px;
726	  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
727	  font-weight: 700;
728	  color: var(--accent);
729	  opacity: 0.9;
730	}
731	.cta-cmd {
732	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
733	  font-size: 16px;
734	  font-weight: 700;
735	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
736	  color: #04121a;
737	  padding: 5px 14px;
738	  border-radius: 7px;
739	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
740	  box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
741	}
742	.cta-key {
743	  display: inline-grid;
744	  place-items: center;
745	  min-width: 26px;
746	  height: 26px;
747	  padding: 0 7px;
748	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
749	  font-size: 14px;
750	  font-weight: 700;
751	  border-radius: 6px;
752	  color: #04121a;
753	}
754	.cta-key-yes {
755	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
756	  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
757	}
758	.cta-key-no {
759	  background: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.16);
760	  color: var(--ink);
761	  border: 1px solid rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.3);
762	}
763	.cta-note {
764	  font-size: 12.5px;
765	  color: var(--ink);
766	  opacity: 0.82;
767	  margin-right: 8px;
768	}
769	
770	/* `entire checkpoint list` output */
771	#term-list:not(.hidden) {
772	  display: flex;
773	  flex-direction: column;
774	  gap: 5px;
775	  margin-top: 11px;
776	  padding-top: 10px;
777	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
778	}
779	.term-list-row {
780	  display: flex;
781	  gap: 12px;
782	  font-size: 12.5px;
783	  line-height: 1.3;
784	}
785	.tl-id { color: var(--accent); min-width: 300px; white-space: nowrap; }
786	.tl-key { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.55; }
787	.tl-title { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.82; }
788	
789	.term-cursor {
790	  display: inline-block;
791	  width: 8px;
792	  height: 16px;
793	  background: var(--accent);
794	  animation: term-blink 1s steps(1) infinite;
795	}
796	
797	@keyframes term-blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
798	
799	#term-msg {
800	  min-height: 13px;
801	  margin-top: 9px;
802	  font-size: 11.5px;
803	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
804	  opacity: 0;
805	  transition: opacity 0.15s ease;
806	}
807	#term-msg.show-ok { opacity: 0.92; color: #7cffb0; }
808	#term-msg.show-err { opacity: 0.92; color: #ff8a73; }
809	
810	/* Map pin marking the landable island */
811	#island-pin {
812	  position: fixed;
813	  top: 0; left: 0;
814	  transform: translate(-50%, -100%);
815	  padding: 0;
816	  background: none;
817	  border: none;
818	  cursor: pointer;
819	  pointer-events: auto;
820	  z-index: 15;
821	  transition: filter 0.2s ease, transform 0.18s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
822	  transform-origin: bottom center;
823	  filter:
824	    drop-shadow(0 2px 4px var(--pin-shadow))
825	    drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.3));
826	}
827	#island-pin .pin-svg { display: block; }
828	#island-pin .pin-svg path {
829	  fill: var(--pin-shell);
830	  stroke: var(--pin-rim);
831	  stroke-width: 1.35;
832	}
833	#island-pin .pin-svg circle {
834	  fill: var(--pin-core);
835	}
836	/* Hover (or keyboard focus, or synced from the 3D region) lifts the pin. */
837	#island-pin:hover,
838	#island-pin:focus-visible,
839	#island-pin.is-hot {
840	  transform: translate(-50%, -100%) scale(1.12);
841	  filter:
842	    drop-shadow(0 3px 5px var(--pin-shadow))
843	    drop-shadow(0 0 12px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.62));
844	  outline: none;
845	}
846	
847	/* Crossfade overlay between views */
848	#fade {
849	  position: fixed;
850	  inset: 0;
851	  background: #07050f;
852	  opacity: 0;
853	  pointer-events: none;
854	  z-index: 40;
855	  transition: opacity 0.65s ease;
856	}
857	#fade.show { opacity: 1; }
858	
859	/* First-person island HUD */
860	#crosshair {
861	  position: absolute;
862	  top: 50%;
863	  left: 50%;
864	  width: 10px;
865	  height: 10px;
866	  margin: -5px 0 0 -5px;
867	  border: 1.5px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.7);
868	  border-radius: 50%;
869	  box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.6);
870	}
871	
872	#fp-prompt {
873	  position: absolute;
874	  bottom: 138px;
875	  left: 50%;
876	  transform: translateX(-50%);
877	  width: max-content;
878	  max-width: calc(100vw - 36px);
879	  padding: 12px 22px;
880	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.7);
881	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);
882	  border-radius: 6px;
883	  font-size: 14px;
884	  line-height: 1.45;
885	  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
886	  color: var(--ink);
887	  text-align: center;
888	  text-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);
889	}
890	
891	#fp-controls {
892	  position: absolute;
893	  right: 28px;
894	  bottom: 28px;
895	  max-width: min(720px, calc(100vw - 56px));
896	  padding: 9px 12px;
897	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.54);
898	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.18);
899	  border-radius: 6px;
900	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.72);
901	  font-size: 10px;
902	  line-height: 1;
903	  letter-spacing: 0;
904	}
905	
906	#fp-controls:not(.hidden) {
907	  display: flex;
908	  flex-wrap: wrap;
909	  align-items: center;
910	  justify-content: flex-end;
911	  gap: 10px 14px;
912	}
913	
914	.control-item {
915	  display: inline-flex;
916	  align-items: center;
917	  gap: 7px;
918	  min-height: 28px;
919	  white-space: nowrap;
920	}
921	
922	.control-label {
923	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.68);
924	  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
925	}
926	
927	.key {
928	  display: inline-flex;
929	  align-items: center;
930	  justify-content: center;
931	  min-width: 20px;
932	  height: 20px;
933	  padding: 0 5px;
934	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.26);
935	  border-radius: 4px;
936	  background: rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.08);
937	  color: var(--ink);
938	  font-size: 10px;
939	  font-weight: 600;
940	  line-height: 1;
941	  box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.4);
942	}
943	
944	.key-wide {
945	  min-width: 30px;
946	}
947	
948	.arrow-keys {
949	  display: grid;
950	  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 18px);
951	  grid-template-rows: repeat(2, 18px);
952	  gap: 2px;
953	}
954	
955	.arrow-keys .key {
956	  min-width: 18px;
957	  width: 18px;
958	  height: 18px;
959	  padding: 0;
960	  font-size: 9px;
961	}
962	
963	.key-up { grid-column: 2; grid-row: 1; }
964	.key-left { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 2; }
965	.key-down { grid-column: 2; grid-row: 2; }
966	.key-right { grid-column: 3; grid-row: 2; }
967	
968	.mouse-hint {
969	  display: inline-flex;
970	  align-items: center;
971	  gap: 6px;
972	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.82);
973	}
974	
975	.mouse-icon {
976	  position: relative;
977	  width: 13px;
978	  height: 19px;
979	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.5);
980	  border-radius: 8px;
981	  background: rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.08);
982	}
983	
984	.mouse-icon::before {
985	  content: "";
986	  position: absolute;
987	  top: 4px;
988	  left: 50%;
989	  width: 2px;
990	  height: 5px;
991	  transform: translateX(-50%);
992	  border-radius: 2px;
993	  background: rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.72);
994	}
995	
996	@media (max-width: 680px) {
997	  #hint {
998	    bottom: 72px;
999	    max-width: calc(100vw - 36px);
1000	    text-align: center;
1001	    white-space: normal;
1002	  }
1003	
1004	  #audio-panel {
1005	    top: auto;
1006	    right: auto;
1007	    bottom: 22px;
1008	    left: 18px;
1009	    gap: 6px;
1010	    padding: 7px 8px;
1011	  }
1012

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/planetfall-seed-signalkit/src/memoryProps.js",
  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\n\n// Shared props for the memory levels: the findable light beam, the ice block a\n// commit freezes a memory into, and the floating `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>`\n// trailer sprite. Used by Level 1 (islandView) and Level 2 (corruptionView).\n\nexport function makeBeamTexture() {\n  const c = document.createElement(\"canvas\");\n  c.width = 8; c.height = 128;\n  const ctx = c.getContext(\"2d\");\n  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);\n  g.addColorStop(0, \"rgba(255,210,122,0)\");\n  g.addColorStop(1, \"rgba(255,210,122,0.9)\");\n  ctx.fillStyle = g;\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 128);\n  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);\n  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\n  return t;\n}\n\n// Translucent crystal that encases a memory once it's committed.\nexport function makeIceBlock() {\n  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.4, 0);\n  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({\n    color: 0xbfe9ff,\n    metalness: 0,\n    roughness: 0.06,\n    transmission: 0.5,\n    thickness: 2.6,\n    ior: 1.3,\n    transparent: true,\n    opacity: 0.62,\n    emissive: 0x0a2230,\n    emissiveIntensity: 0.35,\n    flatShading: true,\n  });\n  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);\n}\n\n// Floating label above a frozen block — the commit trailer Entire stamps on:\n//   \"Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hash>\" (dim key + bright hash).\nexport function makeIdSprite(id) {\n  const c = document.createElement(\"canvas\");\n  c.width = 768; c.height = 64;\n  const ctx = c.getContext(\"2d\");\n  ctx.font = \"bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace\";\n  ctx.textBaseline = \"middle\";\n  ctx.shadowColor = \"rgba(111,227,255,0.9)\";\n  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;\n\n  const key = \"Entire-Checkpoint: \";\n  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;\n  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;\n  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2;       // center the whole trailer\n  ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n  ctx.fillStyle = \"#8fc4dc\";                    // dim trailer key\n  ctx.fillText(key, x, 34);\n  ctx.fillStyle = \"#dff3ff\";                    // bright hash\n  ctx.fillText(id, x + keyW, 34);\n\n  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);\n  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\n  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({\n    map: tex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false,\n  }));\n  spr.scale.set(30, 2.5, 1);                    // 12:1 canvas → keep text height\n  return spr;\n}\n\n// Entire checkpoints get a 12-character hex id (e.g. 711044b1fe29).\nexport function genCheckpointId() {\n  const hex = \"0123456789abcdef\";\n  let s = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) s += hex[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];\n  return s;\n}\n"
}

6	// The walkable island level — LEVEL 1 ("First Memories").
7	//
8	// A Diner-Dash-style loop, one memory at a time, driven by the REAL workflow
9	// typed into the ship's terminal:
10	//   walk up → terminal opens → `git add` (stage) → `git commit` (freeze in ice)
11	//   → Entire offers to link a checkpoint → press y → ship memory restores.
12	// When every memory is linked → run `entire checkpoint list` to review them all.
13	//
14	// PRESSURE: a single level countdown runs the whole time. Bank every memory
15	// before it hits zero, or the un-banked memories melt away and the run fails
16	// (press R to retry). One clock for the whole level — not per-memory.
17	//
18	// Teaching: a commit just freezes the change; linking a CHECKPOINT (the y/n
19	// offer Entire makes after a commit) is what restores it to the ship's memory.
20	
21	const INTERACT_DIST = 8;    // how close (XZ) to open a memory's terminal
22	const TOTAL_TIME = 45;      // seconds to recover & bank EVERY memory (tunable — scary-tight)
23	const MELT_DUR = 1.8;       // seconds the lost memories take to melt away
24	const LOW_TIME = 22;        // clock turns urgent (red, pulsing) under this many seconds
25	const CRIT_TIME = 8;        // clock goes CRITICAL (fast pulse) under this many seconds
26	const PANIC_TIME = 22;      // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red under this many seconds
27	const MEMORY_COUNT = 3;     // calm tutorial: three memories
28	const BEAM_COLOR = 0xffd27a;
29	
30	// Sky panic palette — the whole world reddens as the clock runs out.
31	const SKY_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350);  // lavender dusk (the resting sky/fog)
32	const SKY_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x6e0f16);  // angry crimson
33	const FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);  // deep blood fog closing in
34	const DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);
35	const DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);
36	const SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);  // warm sun
37	const SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);  // hot red alarm light
38	
39	// Where each memory surfaces on the island (world XZ).
40	const PLACEMENTS = [
41	  { x: -34, z: -28 },

48	// What the terminal asks for at each stage of a memory's recovery.
49	const STEPS = {
50	  dormant:   { type: "command", cmd: "git add",    accept: ["git add"],    hint: "Stage the recovered memory" },
51	  recovered: { type: "command", cmd: "git commit", accept: ["git commit"], hint: "Commit it before the clock runs out" },
52	  frozen:    { type: "confirm", question: "Link this commit to a checkpoint?",
53	               hint: "Entire offers to capture the session behind this commit" },
54	};
55	// After every memory is linked, the player reviews them with this command.
56	const REVIEW_STEP = {

58	  hint: "All memories recovered — review what you've banked",
59	};

 # Planetfall
 
-A stranded astronaut whose ship's AI has lost its memory. To escape, you
-explore a 3D planet, find debris, and **recover lost context** — the same act
-that real [Entire](https://docs.entire.io) commands perform on a codebase
-(`recap`, `checkpoint`, `activity`, `dispatch`).
+Planetfall is a 3D browser game about a stranded astronaut whose ship AI has
+lost its memory. To get home, the player restores scattered ship memories while
+learning the real [Entire](https://docs.entire.io) checkpoint workflow:
 
-This is the **3D world prototype**: an explorable, realistically textured
-planet with clickable debris that seed the future context-recovery challenges.
-The challenge mechanics and win/lose flow come next.
+```text
+git add -> git commit -> link the commit to an Entire checkpoint
+```
+
+The current build is Level 1, **First Memories**. It teaches the difference
+between a commit and a checkpoint through a simple game loop: recover a memory,
+freeze it in ice with `git commit`, then bank it into the ship's memory by
+accepting Entire's checkpoint link.
 
 ## Run
 
 ```bash
 npm install
-npm run dev      # http://localhost:5173
+npm run dev
+```
+
+Open <http://localhost:5173>.
+
+Useful commands:
+

60	
61	function normalizeCmd(s) {
62	  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
63	}
64	function cmdMatches(input, accepts) {
65	  const n = normalizeCmd(input);
66	  return accepts.some((a) => n === a || n.startsWith(a + " "));
67	}

69	function makeBeamTexture() {
70	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
71	  c.width = 8; c.height = 128;
72	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
73	  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);
74	  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,210,122,0)");
75	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,210,122,0.9)");
76	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
77	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 128);
78	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
79	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
80	  return t;
81	}

83	// Translucent crystal that encases a memory once it's committed.
84	function makeIceBlock() {
85	  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.4, 0);
86	  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
87	    color: 0xbfe9ff,
88	    metalness: 0,
89	    roughness: 0.06,
90	    transmission: 0.5,
91	    thickness: 2.6,
92	    ior: 1.3,
93	    transparent: true,
94	    opacity: 0.62,
95	    emissive: 0x0a2230,
96	    emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
97	    flatShading: true,
98	  });
99	  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
100	}

102	// Floating label above a frozen block — the commit trailer Entire stamps on:
103	//   "Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hash>" (dim key + bright hash).
104	function makeIdSprite(id) {
105	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
106	  c.width = 768; c.height = 64;
107	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
108	  ctx.font = "bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace";
109	  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
110	  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(111,227,255,0.9)";
111	  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
112	
113	  const key = "Entire-Checkpoint: ";
114	  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;
115	  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;
116	  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2;       // center the whole trailer
117	  ctx.textAlign = "left";
118	  ctx.fillStyle = "#8fc4dc";                    // dim trailer key
119	  ctx.fillText(key, x, 34);
120	  ctx.fillStyle = "#dff3ff";                    // bright hash
121	  ctx.fillText(id, x + keyW, 34);
122	
123	  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
124	  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
125	  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
126	    map: tex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
127	  }));
128	  spr.scale.set(30, 2.5, 1);                    // 12:1 canvas → keep text height
129	  return spr;
130	}
131	
132	// Entire checkpoints get a 12-character hex id (e.g. 711044b1fe29).
133	function genCheckpointId() {
134	  const hex = "0123456789abcdef";
135	  let s = "";
136	  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) s += hex[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
137	  return s;
138	}

275	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
276	  function setPrompt(text) {

284	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
285	  function fmtTime(s) {
286	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
287	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
288	  }
289	  function updateClock() {
290	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
291	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
292	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
293	  }
294	
295	  // How hard the sky is panicking right now: 0 = calm lavender, 1 = full crimson throb.
296	  function panicFactor() {
297	    if (failed) return 1;                         // hold the dread behind the fail screen
298	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
299	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME; // 0 at PANIC_TIME → 1 at 0:00
300	    p *= p;                                        // eased: barely there early, harsh late
301	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {                   // throb once it's critical
302	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
303	      p += 0.14 * throb;
304	    }
305	    return Math.min(1, p);
306	  }
307	  function applyPanicSky() {
308	    const p = panicFactor();
309	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
310	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
311	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
312	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
313	  }
314	  function failLevel() {
315	    if (failed) return;
316	    failed = true;
317	    timerRunning = false;
318	    closeTerminal(false);
319	    fp.detach();                  // freeze the player behind the fail screen
320	    // Un-banked memories always melt; if they ran out the clock on the final
321	    // `entire checkpoint list` (all banked, not yet reviewed), the whole run melts.
322	    for (const a of artifacts) {
323	      if (reviewMode || a.state !== "checkpointed") { a.melting = true; a.meltT = MELT_DUR; }
324	    }
325	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
326	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
327	    levelFail?.classList.remove("hidden");
328	  }
329	  function resetLevel() {
330	    failed = false;
331	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
332	    if (active) fp.attach();      // movement resumes for the new run
333	    for (const a of artifacts) {
334	      if (a.ice) { a.anchor.remove(a.ice); a.ice = null; }
335	      if (a.idSprite) { a.anchor.remove(a.idSprite); a.idSprite = null; }
336	      if (a.core) { a.anchor.remove(a.core); a.core = null; }
337	      a.state = "dormant";
338	      a.id = null;
339	      a.melting = false; a.meltT = 0;
340	      a.model.position.y = 1.4;
341	      setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
342	    }
343	    reviewMode = false;
344	    listShown = false;
345	    dismissedMemory = null;
346	    shipMeter?.classList.remove("is-full");
347	    setPower();
348	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
349	    timerRunning = true;
350	    updateClock();
351	    showTutorial("New attempt — bank all three memories before the clock hits zero.", 4500);
352	  }

443	    // Vertical gauge — fills bottom-up and shifts color as memory is restored.

445	    if (shipMeterPct) shipMeterPct.textContent = pct + "%";
446	    if (shipMeter) {

448	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-mid", pct > 34 && pct < 100);

466	    controlsLocked = locked;
467	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
468	      <span class="control-item">
469	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
470	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
471	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
472	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
473	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
474	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
475	        </span>
476	      </span>
477	      ${locked ? `
478	        <span class="control-item">
479	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
480	          <span class="mouse-hint">
481	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
482	            <span>move mouse</span>
483	          </span>
484	        </span>
485	      ` : ""}
486	      <span class="control-item">
487	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
488	        <span class="key">B</span>
489	      </span>
490	    `;
491	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
492	  }
493	  function hideControls() {
494	    controlsLocked = null;
495	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
496	  }
497	
498	  function setModelOpacity(model, o) {
499	    model.traverse((n) => {
500	      if (n.material) { n.material.transparent = o < 1; n.material.opacity = o; }
501	    });
502	  }
503	
504	  // ---------- terminal ----------
505	  function currentStep() {
506	    if (reviewMode) return REVIEW_STEP;
507	    if (!termTarget) return null;
508	    return STEPS[termTarget.state] || null;
509	  }
510	  function renderTerminal() {
511	    const step = currentStep();
512	    if (!step) return;
513	
514	    if (reviewMode && listShown) {
515	      termHint.textContent = "# entire checkpoint list";
516	      termInput.textContent = "entire checkpoint list";
517	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
518	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = "";
519	      termList?.classList.remove("hidden");
520	      return;
521	    }
522	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
523	
524	    // The hint is just the "why"; the actionable command/keys live in the CTA
525	    // below the input line, where they're the most prominent thing on screen.
526	    termHint.textContent = step.hint ? `# ${step.hint}` : "";
527	    if (step.type === "confirm") {
528	      termInput.textContent = `${step.question}  [y/n]`;
529	      termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
530	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
531	        `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
532	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd><span class="cta-note">link checkpoint</span>` +
533	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-no">N</kbd><span class="cta-note">skip</span>`;
534	    } else {
535	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
536	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
537	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
538	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">${step.cmd}</span>`;
539	    }
540	  }
541	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
542	    if (!termMsg) return;
543	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
544	    termMsg.textContent = text;
545	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
546	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
547	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 2400);
548	  }
549	  function openTerminal(a) {
550	    if (terminalOpen) return;
551	    terminalOpen = true;
552	    termTarget = a;
553	    buffer = "";
554	    fp.detach();                      // freeze movement; keystrokes feed the terminal
555	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
556	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
557	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
558	    renderTerminal();
559	  }
560	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
561	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
562	    if (dismiss && termTarget) dismissedMemory = termTarget;
563	    terminalOpen = false;
564	    termTarget = null;
565	    buffer = "";
566	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
567	    if (active) fp.attach();          // resume walking (addEventListener dedupes)
568	  }
569	  function submitCommand() {
570	    const step = currentStep();
571	    if (!step || step.type !== "command" || listShown) return;
572	    if (cmdMatches(buffer, step.accept)) {
573	      buffer = "";
574	      if (reviewMode) { showCheckpointList(); return; }
575	      runStep(termTarget);            // dormant → stage, recovered → commit
576	      flashTerminal("✓ ok", true);
577	      renderTerminal();
578	    } else {
579	      flashTerminal(`command not recognized — try:  ${step.cmd}`, false);
580	      buffer = "";
581	      renderTerminal();
582	    }
583	  }
584	  function showCheckpointList() {
585	    listShown = true;
586	    timerRunning = false;            // the run is complete — NOW the clock stops
587	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
588	    if (termList) {
589	      termList.innerHTML = artifacts.map((a) =>
590	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id">` +
591	        `<span class="tl-key">Entire-Checkpoint:</span> ${a.id}</span>` +
592	        `<span class="tl-title">recovered: ${a.fragment.title}</span></div>`
593	      ).join("");
594	    }
595	    flashTerminal(`${artifacts.length} checkpoints linked · ship memory restored`, true);
596	    showTutorial("Memory restored — the ship knows the way home. Press Esc to close the terminal, then B to return to orbit.", 0);
597	    renderTerminal();
598	  }
599	
600	  // ---------- state transitions ----------
601	  function runStep(a) {
602	    if (a.state === "dormant") stage(a);
603	    else if (a.state === "recovered") commit(a);
604	  }
605	  function stage(a) {
606	    a.state = "recovered";
607	    teachOnce("staged",
608	      "Recovered and staged. Run `git commit` to freeze it — the clock is ticking.");
609	  }
610	  function commit(a) {
611	    a.state = "frozen";
612	    setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
613	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
614	    ice.position.y = a.model.position.y + 0.6;
615	    a.iceY = ice.position.y;
616	    a.anchor.add(ice);
617	    a.ice = ice;
618	    teachOnce("committed",
619	      "Committed and frozen — locked in. Now Entire offers to link a checkpoint: press y to capture the session behind this commit.");
620	  }
621	  function confirmCheckpoint(yes) {
622	    const a = termTarget;
623	    if (!a || a.state !== "frozen") return;
624	    if (!yes) {
625	      flashTerminal("declined — without a checkpoint the ship can't track this commit. re-approach to link it.", false);
626	      closeTerminal(true);
627	      return;
628	    }
629	    checkpoint(a);
630	    if (allLinked()) {
631	      reviewMode = true;             // clock KEEPS running — the list is the finish line
632	      shipMeter?.classList.add("is-full");
633	      showTutorial("All banked! Now type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00 — hurry!", 0);
634	      renderTerminal();              // stay open → review step
635	    } else {
636	      closeTerminal(false);
637	    }
638	  }
639	  function checkpoint(a) {
640	    a.state = "checkpointed";
641	    a.id = genCheckpointId();
642	    const shipPct = 62 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 23); // 62–84%
643	
644	    if (a.ice) {
645	      a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);
646	      a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
647	      const core = new THREE.PointLight(0x8fe3ff, 6, 26, 2);
648	      core.position.copy(a.ice.position);
649	      a.anchor.add(core);
650	      a.core = core;
651	    }
652	    const spr = makeIdSprite(a.id);
653	    spr.position.y = a.iceY + 5;
654	    a.anchor.add(spr);
655	    a.idSprite = spr;
656	
657	    showCard(a, shipPct);
658	    setPower();
659	    teachOnce("checkpointed",
660	      "Checkpoint linked — Entire stamps it onto the commit via an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer, and the ship remembers a little more.");
661	  }
662	
663	  // ---------- input ----------
664	  function onCanvasClick() {
665	    if (!started) return;                 // briefing up — ignore world clicks
666	    if (active && !fp.isLocked) fp.lock();
667	  }
668	  function onKeyDown(e) {
669	    if (!active) return;
670	
671	    // Landing briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
672	    if (!started) {
673	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
674	      return;
675	    }
676	
677	    // The run failed — only R (retry) does anything.
678	    if (failed) {
679	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
680	      return;
681	    }
682	
683	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
684	    if (terminalOpen) {
685	      if (e.code === "Escape") {
686	        // No bailing on the final review — the clock is still ticking.
687	        if (reviewMode && !listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
688	        closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return;
689	      }
690	      if (reviewMode && listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; } // only Esc closes the list
691	
692	      const step = currentStep();
693	      if (step?.type === "confirm") {
694	        if (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y") { confirmCheckpoint(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
695	        if (e.key === "n" || e.key === "N") { confirmCheckpoint(false); e.preventDefault(); return; }
696	        e.preventDefault();
697	        return;
698	      }
699	      // command mode
700	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
701	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
702	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
703	        buffer += e.key;
704	        renderTerminal();
705	        e.preventDefault();
706	      }
707	      return;
708	    }
709	
710	    // Walking around (terminal closed).
711	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
712	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
713	  }
714	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
715	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
716	
717	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
718	  function refreshHud() {
719	    if (!started || terminalOpen) {     // briefing up or terminal open → no walk HUD
720	      setPrompt(null);
721	      hideControls();
722	      hideActionBar();
723	      return;
724	    }
725	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
726	    hideActionBar();
727	    if (devLevel2) {
728	      setPrompt(fp.isLocked ? "Level 2 dev start — press B to return to orbit" : "Click to look around");
729	      return;
730	    }
731	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
732	    if (listShown) { setPrompt("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit"); return; }
733	    setPrompt("Find the surfacing memory");
734	  }
735	
736	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
737	  function update(dt, t) {
738	    fp.update(dt);
739	
740	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
741	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed) {
742	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
743	      updateClock();
744	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
745	    }
746	    applyPanicSky();   // the sky reacts to the clock every frame
747	
748	    // Nearest memory within reach (ignoring height).
749	    let nearest = null, nd = Infinity;
750	    for (const a of artifacts) {
751	      const dx = camera.position.x - a.anchor.position.x;
752	      const dz = camera.position.z - a.anchor.position.z;
753	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
754	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; nearest = a; }
755	    }
756	    target = nd <= INTERACT_DIST ? nearest : null;
757	
758	    // Auto open/close the ship's terminal as you reach a memory.
759	    // (In review mode the terminal is driven manually, so leave it alone.)
760	    if (active && started && !reviewMode && !failed && !devLevel2) {
761	      if (dismissedMemory && target !== dismissedMemory) dismissedMemory = null;
762	      const canOpen = target && target.state !== "checkpointed" && target !== dismissedMemory;
763	      if (canOpen && !terminalOpen) openTerminal(target);
764	      else if (!canOpen && terminalOpen) closeTerminal(false);
765	    }
766	
767	    for (const a of artifacts) {
768	      // Melting away after a time-out — sink and fade, then disappear.
769	      if (a.melting) {
770	        a.meltT = Math.max(0, a.meltT - dt);
771	        const k = a.meltT / MELT_DUR;
772	        setModelOpacity(a.model, k);
773	        a.model.position.y -= dt * 2.4;
774	        if (a.ice) a.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * k;
775	        a.beam.visible = false;
776	        a.glow.visible = false;
777	        continue;
778	      }
779	
780	      const encased = a.state === "frozen" || a.state === "checkpointed";
781	
782	      // Idle spin/bob until the memory is encased in ice.
783	      if (!encased) {
784	        a.model.rotation.y += dt * 0.6;
785	        a.model.position.y = 1.4 + Math.sin((t + a.bobOff) * 1.5) * 0.4;
786	        setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
787	      }
788	
789	      // Beam + glow: bright while findable, gone once frozen.
790	      const lit = a.state === "dormant" || a.state === "recovered";
791	      let o = 0;
792	      if (a.state === "dormant") o = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.12;
793	      else if (a.state === "recovered") o = 0.22;
794	      a.beam.material.opacity = o;
795	      a.beam.visible = o > 0.01;
796	      a.glow.visible = lit;
797	    }
798	
799	    if (terminalOpen) renderTerminal(); // reflect fade-driven state changes
800	    if (active) refreshHud();
801	  }
802	
803	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
804	  function enter() {
805	    active = true;
806	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
807	    camera.lookAt(0, terrain.heightAt(0, 0) + 6, 0);
808	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
809	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
810	    islandHud?.classList.remove("hidden");
811	    islandHud?.classList.toggle("is-level2-dev", devLevel2);
812	    devLevelBadge?.classList.toggle("hidden", !devLevel2);
813	    setControls(false);
814	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
815	    setPower();
816	    if (devLevel2) {
817	      seedLevel2DevState();
818	      fp.attach();
819	      showTutorial("Level 2 dev mode — Level 1 checkpoints are preloaded.", 0);
820	    } else if (!started) {          // fresh landing — read the briefing, then START
821	      showBriefing();
822	    } else if (listShown) {         // truly done — the list has been run
823	      fp.attach();
824	      timerRunning = false;
825	      showTutorial("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit.", 0);
826	    } else if (failed) {
827	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
828	    } else {
829	      fp.attach();
830	      timerRunning = true;          // resume the level clock
831	      if (reviewMode) {             // resume the timed final review
832	        openTerminal(artifacts[0]); // termTarget unused in review; reopens the prompt
833	        showTutorial("Type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00!", 0);
834	      } else {
835	        showTutorial("Memories are surfacing — the clock's short. Bank all three before 0:00!", 6500);
836	      }
837	    }
838	    updateClock();
839	    applyPanicSky();   // start on the right sky (calm unless we resumed low on time)
840	  }
841	  function exit() {
842	    active = false;
843	    timerRunning = false;           // pause the clock while in orbit
844	    closeTerminal(false);
845	    fp.unlock();
846	    fp.detach();
847	    canvas.removeEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
848	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
849	    setPrompt(null);
850	    hideControls();
851	    hideActionBar();
852	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
853	    ckptCard?.classList.add("hidden");
854	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
855	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
856	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
857	    islandHud?.classList.add("hidden");
858	    islandHud?.classList.remove("is-level2-dev");
859	    devLevelBadge?.classList.add("hidden");
860	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
861	  }
862	  function resize() {
863	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
864	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
865	  }
866	
867	  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };
868	}
869

c.height = 64;\n  const ctx = c.getContext(\"2d\");\n  ctx.font = \"bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace\";\n  ctx.textBaseline = \"middle\";\n  ctx.shadowColor = \"rgba(111,227,255,0.9)\";\n  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;\n\n  const key = \"Entire-Checkpoint: \";\n  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;\n  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;\n  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2; color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.3,\n    })\n  );\n  screen.position.set(0, 4.0, 0.82);\n  screen.rotation.x = -0.28;\n  g.add(pedestal, screen);\n  g.userData.screen = screen;\n  return g;\n}\n\nexport function createCorruptionView(renderer, { onExit } = {}) {\n  const canvas = renderer.domElement;\n\n  // ---------- scene & sky ----------\n  const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n  scene.background = SKY_SICK.clone();\n  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(FOG_SICK.clone(), 90, 320);\n\n  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(\n    62, const anchor = new THREE.Group();\n    anchor.position.set(place.x, groundY, place.z);\n\n    const model = BUILDERS[clue.kind]();\n    model.scale.setScalar(14);\n    model.position.y = 1.4;\n    anchor.add(model);\n\n    const ice = makeIceBlock();\n    ice.position.y = 2.0;\n    ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);\n    ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;\n    anchor.add(ice);\n\n anchor.add(glow);\n\n    scene.add(anchor);\n    artifacts.push({\n      anchor, model, ice, glow, clue,\n      iceBaseX: ice.position.x, iceBaseY: ice.position.y,\n      distFromOrigin: consoleAnchor.add(consoleProp);\n\n  // A cyan beam so the terminal is findable from anywhere on the island.\n  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();\n  const consoleBeam = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      map: beamTex, color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4,\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, Math.hypot(ORIGIN.x - CONSOLE_POS.x, ORIGIN.z - CONSOLE_POS.z) - 2;\n\n  // ---------- corruption motes (glitch particles drifting over the wound) ----------\n  const MOTES = 260;\n  const moteFrac = new Float32Array(MOTES);   // 0..1 of the current radius\n  const moteAng = new Float32Array(MOTES);\n  const motePhase = new Float32Array(MOTES);\n  for (let i = 0; i < MOTES; i++) {\n    moteFrac[i] = Math.pow(Math.random(), 0.6);\n    moteAng[i] = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    motePhase[i] = Math.random() * 10;\n  }\n  const moteGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  moteGeo.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(MOTES * 3), 3));\n  const motes = new THREE.Points(moteGeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n    color: 0xff2bd6, size: 0.7, = () => [...CHECKPOINTS].sort((a, b) => (a.time < b.time ? 1 : -1));\n\n  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------\n  function setPrompt(text) {\n    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;\n    promptText = text;\n    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\"); }\n    else promptEl.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500)       <span class=\"key\">B</span>\n      </span>\n    `;\n    controlsEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function hideControls() {\n    controlsLocked = null;\n    controlsEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- corruption meter ----------\n  function corruptionPct() {\n    return Math.min(100, Math.round(\n      ((radius - START_RADIUS) / (FAIL_RADIUS - START_RADIUS)) * 100\n    ));\n  }\n  function updateMeter() {\n    const pct = restored ? 0 : corruptionPct();\n    if (corruptFill) corruptFill.style.width = pct + \"%\";\n    if (corruptPct) corruptPct.textContent = pct + \"%\";\n    corruptMeter?.classList.toggle(\"is-high\", !restored && pct >= 70);\n    if (clueCount) clueCount.textContent = `${cluesFound()} / ${artifacts.length}`;\n  }\n\n  // ---------- the spreading wound ----------\n  // Repaint terrain vertex colors around the front every frame: consumed land\n  // collapses to void, with a flickering magenta band at the leading edge.\n  function paintCorruption(t) {\n    const cols = colAttr.array;\n    const flicker = 0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 9);\n    const reach = radius + EDGE_BAND;\n    for (let v = 0; v < posAttr.count; v++) {\n      const d = vertDist[v];\n      const i = v * 3;\n      if (d >= reach) {\n        if (cols[i] !== baseColors[i] || cols[i + 1] !== baseColors[i + 1]) {\n          cols[i] = baseColors[i];\n          cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1];\n          cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2];\n        }\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (d <= radius) {\n        // Inside the wound — sink to void, deeper toward the origin.\n        const k = Math.min(1, (radius - d) / 10 + 0.45);\n        cols[i] = baseColors[i] + (C_VOID[0] - baseColors[i]) * k;\n        cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1] + (C_VOID[1] - baseColors[i + 1]) * k;\n        cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2] + (C_VOID[2] - baseColors[i + 2]) * k;\n      } else {\n        // The leading edge — a thin magenta burn.\n        const k = (1 - (d - radius) / EDGE_BAND) * flicker;\n        cols[i] = baseColors[i] + (C_EDGE[0] - baseColors[i]) * k;\n        cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1] + (C_EDGE[1] - baseColors[i + 1]) * k;\n        cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2] + (C_EDGE[2] - baseColors[i + 2]) * k;\n      }\n    }\n    colAttr.needsUpdate = true;\n  }\n\n  function updateMotes(t) {\n    const arr = moteGeo.attributes.position.array;\n    for (let i = 0; i < MOTES; i++) {\n      const r = moteFrac[i] * radius;\n      const x = ORIGIN.x + Math.cos(moteAng[i] + t * 0.05) * r;\n      const z = ORIGIN.z + Math.sin(moteAng[i] + t * 0.05) * r;\n      arr[i * 3] = x;\n      arr[i * 3 + 1] = terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 1.2 +\n        Math.sin(t * 1.6 + motePhase[i]) * 1.0 + ((t * 0.8 + motePhase[i]) % 3);\n      arr[i * 3 + 2] = z;\n    }\n    moteGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n    motes.material.opacity = restored ? 0 : 0.45 + 0.3 * Math.sin(t * 7);\n  }\n\n  // Sky drains toward the void as the corruption nears the terminal; a good\n  // rewind lerps everything back to Level 1's calm lavender.\n  function applySky() {\n    if (restored || restoring) {\n      const k = restoring ? Math.min(1, restoreT / RESTORE_DUR) : 1;\n      scene.background.copy(SKY_SICK).lerp(SKY_HEALED, k);\n      scene.fog.color.copy(FOG_SICK).lerp(SKY_HEALED, k);\n      dome.material.color.copy(DOME_SICK).lerp(DOME_HEALED, k);\n      sun.color.copy(SUN_SICK).lerp(SUN_HEALED, k);\n      return;\n    }\n    let p = corruptionPct() / 100;\n    if (failed) p = 1;\n    p *= p;\n    if (p > 0.55) p += 0.1 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 120));\n    p = Math.min(1, p);\n    scene.background.copy(SKY_SICK).lerp(SKY_VOID, p);\n    scene.fog.color.copy(FOG_SICK).lerp(FOG_VOID, p);\n    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_SICK).lerp(DOME_VOID, p);\n    sun.color.copy(SUN_SICK).lerp(SUN_VOID, p * 0.9);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- briefing / fail / reset ----------\n  function showBriefing() {\n    fp.detach();\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    briefingEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function startLevel() {\n    if (started) return;\n    started = true;\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    fp.attach();\n    showTutorial(\"The corruption is spreading from the nav panel's memory. Inspect the frozen memories (E) — their words are clues.\", 8000);\n  }\n  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener(\"click\", startLevel);\n\n  function failLevel() {\n    if (failed || restored || restoring) return;\n    failed = true;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n    closeClue();\n    fp.detach();\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    failEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function resetLevel() {\n    failed = false;\n    restored = false;\n    restoring = false;\n    restoreT = 0;\n    radius = START_RADIUS;\n    searched = false;\n    lastResults = [];\n    explained.clear();\n    consoleDismissed = false;\n    for (const a of artifacts) a.inspected = false;\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    updateMeter();\n    if (active) fp.attach();\n    showTutorial(\"New attempt — find the last good checkpoint before the corruption reaches the terminal.\", 6000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- clue cards ----------\n  function openClueCard(a) {\n    openClue = a;\n    fp.detach();\n    if (!a.inspected) {\n      a.inspected = true;\n      updateMeter();\n      if (a.clue.origin) {\n        teachOnce(\"anomaly\",\n          `Anomaly time logged — ${ANOMALY}. When you explain a checkpoint, check its time against this.`, 8000);\n      } else {\n        teachOnce(\"clue\",\n          `Clue logged — “${a.clue.key}”. Words like this are searchable from the ship terminal.`, 7000);\n      }\n    }\n    if (clueTitle) clueTitle.textContent = a.clue.title;\n    if (clueBody) clueBody.textContent = a.clue.body;\n    if (clueKey) clueKey.textContent = a.clue.key;\n    clueCard?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function closeClue() {\n    if (!openClue) return;\n    openClue = null;\n    clueCard?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    if (active && !failed && !restored) fp.attach();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- terminal ----------\n  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {\n    if (!termMsg) return;\n    clearTimeout(msgTimer);\n    termMsg.textContent = text;\n    termMsg.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\");\n    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? \"show-ok\" : \"show-err\");\n    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\"), 3600);\n  }\n\n  // The next command the CTA should push the player toward.\n  function suggestedCmd() {\n    if (cluesFound() === 0) return null;\n    if (!searched) {\n      const firstClue = artifacts.find((a) => a.inspected && !a.clue.origin);\n      return `entire checkpoint search \"${firstClue ? firstClue.clue.key : \"course plot\"}\"`;\n    }\n    if (explained.size === 0 && lastResults.length) {\n      return `entire checkpoint explain ${lastResults[0].id}`;\n    }\n    return \"entire checkpoint rewind\";\n  }\n  function renderTerminal() {\n    if (!terminalOpen) return;\n    if (browserMode) return;   // the rewind browser owns the terminal until Esc/Enter\n    if (confirmTarget) {\n      termHint.textContent = \"# rewinding is safe — you can always rewind again\";\n      termInput.textContent = `rewind to ${confirmTarget.id} (${confirmTarget.time})?  [y/n]`;\n      termInput.classList.add(\"is-dim\");\n      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n        `<span class=\"cta-label\">PRESS</span>` +\n        `<kbd class=\"cta-key cta-key-yes\">Y</kbd><span class=\"cta-note\">rewind</span>` +\n        `<kbd class=\"cta-key cta-key-no\">N</kbd><span class=\"cta-note\">cancel</span>`;\n      return;\n    }\n    termInput.classList.remove(\"is-dim\");\n    termInput.textContent = buffer;\n    const cmd = suggestedCmd();\n    if (!cmd) {\n      termHint.textContent = \"# the log is searchable — but you need words to search for\";\n      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n        `<span class=\"cta-label\">FIRST</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"cta-note\">inspect the frozen memories — walk up, press E</span>`;\n    } else {\n      const why = !searched\n        ? \"Search the ship's checkpoint log for the words you found\"\n        : explained.size === 0\n          ? \"Explain a candidate — was it written before or after the anomaly?\"\n          : \"Browse the checkpoints and rewind to the last good one\";\n      termHint.textContent = `# ${why}`;\n      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n        `<span class=\"cta-label\">TYPE</span><span class=\"cta-cmd\">${cmd}</span>`;\n    }\n  }\n  function openTerminal() {\n    if (terminalOpen) return;\n    terminalOpen = true;\n    buffer = \"\";\n    fp.detach();\n    termMsg?.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\");\n    termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    teachOnce(\"terminal\",\n      \"The ship terminal reads the checkpoint log — every memory you've ever banked. Search it, explain it, rewind it.\", 7500);\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {\n    if (!terminalOpen) return;\n    if (dismiss) consoleDismissed = true;\n    terminalOpen = false;\n    buffer = \"\";\n    browserMode = false;\n    confirmTarget = null;\n    termEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    if (active && !failed && !restored && !restoring) fp.attach();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- entire checkpoint search ----------\n  function doSearch(raw) {\n    const q = raw.replace(/^.*?search/i, \"\").replace(/[\"']/g, \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n    const words = q.split(/\\s+/).filter((w) => w.length >= 3);\n    if (!words.length) {\n      flashTerminal('search needs words — try:  entire checkpoint search \"course plot\"', false);\n      return;\n    }\n    const scored = CHECKPOINTS\n      .map((cp) => ({\n        cp,\n        score: words.filter((w) => cp.summary.toLowerCase().includes(w)).length,\n      }))\n      .filter((r) => r.score > 0)\n      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || (a.cp.time < b.cp.time ? 1 : -1));\n    if (!scored.length) {\n      flashTerminal(`0 results for “${q}” — try words from the memories you inspected`, false);\n      return;\n    }\n    searched = true;\n    lastResults = scored.map((r) => r.cp);\n    if (termList) {\n      termList.innerHTML = lastResults.map((cp) =>\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-id\">${cp.id}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-time\">${cp.time}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">${cp.summary}</span></div>`\n      ).join(\"\");\n      termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n    flashTerminal(`${lastResults.length} checkpoint${lastResults.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} matched “${q}”`, true);\n    teachOnce(\"searched\",\n      \"Each result is a moment you can return to. `entire checkpoint explain <id>` shows what's inside one.\", 8000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain ----------\n  function doExplain(n) {\n    const token = n.replace(/^.*?explain/i, \"\").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0] || \"\";\n    if (token.length < 4) {\n      flashTerminal(\"explain needs a checkpoint id — copy one from the search results\", false);\n      return;\n    }\n    const cp = CHECKPOINTS.find((c) => c.id.startsWith(token.toLowerCase()));\n    if (!cp) {\n      flashTerminal(`no checkpoint matching “${token}” — copy an id from the search results`, false);\n      return;\n    }\n    explained.add(cp.id);\n    if (termList) {\n      const rows = [\n        [\"checkpoint\", cp.id],\n        [\"time\", cp.time],\n        [\"summary\", cp.summary],\n        [\"files\", cp.files],\n        [\"session\", cp.session],\n      ];\n      termList.innerHTML = rows.map(([k, v]) =>\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">${k}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">${v}</span></div>`\n      ).join(\"\") + (anomalyKnown()\n        ? `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">#</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-anomaly\">anomaly began ${ANOMALY} — is ${cp.time} before or after?</span></div>`\n        : \"\");\n      termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n    flashTerminal(`explained ${cp.id}`, true);\n    teachOnce(\"explained\",\n      \"Explain shows the session behind a checkpoint — when, what, and why. Compare times. Then `entire checkpoint rewind`.\", 8000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- entire checkpoint rewind ----------\n  function openBrowser() {\n    browserMode = true;\n    browserSel = 0;\n    renderBrowser();\n    teachOnce(\"browser\",\n      \"The rewind browser lists every checkpoint, newest first. ↑/↓ to choose, Enter to rewind, Esc to cancel.\", 8000);\n  }\n  function renderBrowser() {\n    termHint.textContent = \"# entire checkpoint rewind — choose where to return to\";\n    termInput.textContent = \"entire checkpoint rewind\";\n    termInput.classList.add(\"is-dim\");\n    if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"cta-label\">KEYS</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"cta-note\">↑/↓ choose · Enter rewind · Esc cancel</span>`;\n    if (termList) {\n      termList.innerHTML = browserList().map((cp, i) =>\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row${i === browserSel ? \" is-sel\" : \"\"}\">` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-sel\">${i === browserSel ? \"▸\" : \" \"}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-id\">${cp.id}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-time\">${cp.time}</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">${cp.summary}</span></div>`\n      ).join(\"\");\n      termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n  }\n  function applyRewind(cp) {\n    confirmTarget = null;\n    termInput.classList.remove(\"is-dim\");\n    if (cp.good) {\n      startRestore(cp);\n      return;\n    }\n    // Soft fail — the wrong moment. The corruption surges, but rewinding is\n    // safe and repeatable: that's the whole lesson.\n    radius = Math.min(radius + SURGE, FAIL_RADIUS + 0.1);\n    glitchFlash?.classList.remove(\"show\");\n    void glitchFlash?.offsetWidth;   // restart the flash animation\n    glitchFlash?.classList.add(\"show\");\n    flashTerminal(\n      `rewound to ${cp.id} — still corrupted. it was written ${cp.time}, after the anomaly. rewind again — rewinding is always safe.`,\n      false\n    );\n    termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    buffer = \"\";\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n  function startRestore(cp) {\n    restoring = true;\n    restoreT = 0;\n    restoreFrom = radius;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n    fp.detach();\n    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = `rewound to ${cp.id} · ${cp.summary}`;\n    showTutorial(`Rewinding to ${cp.id} — ${cp.time}, the last good moment…`, 0);\n  }\n  function finishRestore() {\n    restoring = false;\n    restored = true;\n    radius = 0;\n    updateMeter();\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    if (active) fp.attach();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- command dispatch ----------\n  function submitCommand() {\n    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);\n    buffer = \"\";\n    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }\n    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) search\\b/.test(n)) doSearch(n);\n    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) explain\\b/.test(n)) doExplain(n);\n    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) rewind\\b/.test(n)) { openBrowser(); return; }\n    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {\n      if (termList) {\n        termList.innerHTML = browserList().map((cp) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-id\">${cp.id}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-time\">${cp.time}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">${cp.summary}</span></div>`\n        ).join(\"\");\n        termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n      flashTerminal(\"the full log — search finds the ones that matter\", true);\n    } else {\n      const cmd = suggestedCmd();\n      flashTerminal(`command not recognized${cmd ? ` — try:  ${cmd}` : \" — inspect the memories for clues first\"}`, false);\n    }\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onCanvasClick() {\n    if (!started || failed) return;\n    if (active && !fp.isLocked && !terminalOpen && !openClue) fp.lock();\n  }\n  function onKeyDown(e) {\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.\n    if (!started) {\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\" || e.code === \"Space\") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // The corruption won — only R (retry) does anything.\n    if (failed) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyR\") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Reading a clue — E or Esc puts it down.\n    if (openClue) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyE\" || e.code === \"Escape\") { closeClue(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.\n    if (terminalOpen) {\n      if (confirmTarget) {\n        if (e.key === \"y\" || e.key === \"Y\") { applyRewind(confirmTarget); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n        if (e.key === \"n\" || e.key === \"N\" || e.code === \"Escape\") {\n          confirmTarget = null;\n          openBrowser();             // back to the browser, nothing lost\n          e.preventDefault();\n          return;\n        }\n        e.preventDefault();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (browserMode) {\n        const list = browserList();\n        if (e.code === \"ArrowUp\") { browserSel = (browserSel + list.length - 1) % list.length; renderBrowser(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n        if (e.code === \"ArrowDown\") { browserSel = (browserSel + 1) % list.length; renderBrowser(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n        if (e.code === \"Enter\") {\n          browserMode = false;\n          confirmTarget = list[browserSel];\n          termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n          renderTerminal();\n          e.preventDefault();\n          return;\n        }\n        if (e.code === \"Escape\") {\n          browserMode = false;\n          termInput.classList.remove(\"is-dim\");\n          termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n          renderTerminal();\n          e.preventDefault();\n          return;\n        }\n        e.preventDefault();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (e.code === \"Escape\") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Backspace\") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {\n        buffer += e.key;\n        renderTerminal();\n        e.preventDefault();\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Walking around.\n    if (e.code === \"KeyE\" && nearArtifact) { openClueCard(nearArtifact); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n    if (e.code === \"Escape\" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();\n    if (e.code === \"KeyB\") onExit?.();\n  }\n  fp.controls.addEventListener(\"lock\", () => crosshair?.classList.remove(\"hidden\"));\n  fp.controls.addEventListener(\"unlock\", () => crosshair?.classList.add(\"hidden\"));\n\n  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------\n  let nearArtifact = null;\n  let nearConsole = false;\n\n  function refreshHud() {\n    if (!started || terminalOpen || openClue) {\n      setPrompt(null);\n      hideControls();\n      return;\n    }\n    setControls(fp.isLocked);\n    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt(\"Click to look around\"); return; }\n    if (restored) { setPrompt(\"Timeline restored — press B to return to orbit\"); return; }\n    if (restoring) { setPrompt(null); return; }\n    if (nearArtifact) {\n      setPrompt(nearArtifact.inspected\n        ? \"Press E — review the clue\"\n        : \"Press E — inspect the frozen memory\");\n      return;\n    }\n    const found = cluesFound();\n    if (found === 0) setPrompt(\"Find the frozen memories — their words are clues\");\n    else if (found < artifacts.length) setPrompt(\"More clues remain — or head for the cyan beam: the ship terminal\");\n    else setPrompt(\"Head for the cyan beam — the ship terminal\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- per-frame ----------\n  function update(dt, t) {\n    fp.update(dt);\n\n    // The corruption spreads while the run is live.\n    if (active && started && !failed && !restoring && !restored) {\n      radius += SPREAD_RATE * dt;\n      if (radius >= FAIL_RADIUS) failLevel();\n      updateMeter();\n    }\n\n    // A good rewind pulls the wound back to nothing.\n    if (restoring) {\n      restoreT += dt;\n      const k = Math.min(1, restoreT / RESTORE_DUR);\n      radius = restoreFrom * (1 - k * k);   // accelerating retreat\n      updateMeter();\n      if (restoreT >= RESTORE_DUR) finishRestore();\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n      paintCorruption(t);\n      updateMotes(t);\n      applySky();\n    }\n\n    // Artifacts: gentle glow while safe; glitch-jitter once the wound has them.\n    for (const a of artifacts) {\n      const consumed = a.distFromOrigin <= radius + EDGE_BAND;\n      const sick = a.clue.origin || consumed;\n      if (sick && !restored && !restoring) {\n        const j = a.clue.origin ? 0.22 : 0.12;\n        a.ice.position.x = a.iceBaseX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * j;\n        a.ice.position.y = a.iceBaseY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * j * 0.5;\n        const blink = Math.sin(t * (a.clue.origin ? 14 : 9)) > 0;\n        a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(blink ? 0xa6126e : 0x123a4a);\n        a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.3;\n        a.glow.color.setHex(blink ? 0xff2bd6 : 0x8fe3ff);\n      } else {\n        a.ice.position.x = a.iceBaseX;\n        a.ice.position.y = a.iceBaseY;\n        a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);\n        a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;\n        a.glow.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // The console screen flickers harder as the corruption closes in.\n    const screen = consoleProp.userData.screen;\n    if (screen) {\n      const closeness = Math.min(1, radius / FAIL_RADIUS);\n      const flick = Math.sin(t * (6 + closeness * 18)) > -0.2 + closeness * 0.8;\n      screen.material.emissive.setHex(\n        restored || restoring ? 0x2a8aa6 : flick ? 0x2a8aa6 : 0xa6126e\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Proximity: nearest inspectable memory, and the ship terminal.\n    nearArtifact = null;\n    let nd = Infinity;\n    for (const a of artifacts) {\n      const d = Math.hypot(\n        camera.position.x - a.anchor.position.x,\n        camera.position.z - a.anchor.position.z\n      );\n      if (d < nd) { nd = d; if (d <= INTERACT_DIST) nearArtifact = a; }\n    }\n    nearConsole = Math.hypot(\n      camera.position.x - CONSOLE_POS.x, camera.position.z - CONSOLE_POS.z\n    ) <= CONSOLE_DIST;\n\n    // The terminal opens itself when you reach the console, like Level 1.\n    if (active && started && !failed && !restoring && !restored && !openClue) {\n      if (consoleDismissed && !nearConsole) consoleDismissed = false;\n      if (nearConsole && !terminalOpen && !consoleDismissed) openTerminal();\n      else if (!nearConsole && terminalOpen) closeTerminal(false);\n    }\n\n    if (terminalOpen && !browserMode && !confirmTarget) renderTerminal();\n    if (active) refreshHud();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- lifecycle ----------\n  function enter() {\n    active = true;\n    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);\n    camera.lookAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z) + 5, CONSOLE_POS.z);\n    canvas.addEventListener(\"click\", onCanvasClick);\n    window.addEventListener(\"keydown\", onKeyDown);\n    l2Hud?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    fpShared?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    setControls(false);\n    crosshair?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    updateMeter();\n    if (!started) {\n      showBriefing();\n    } else if (restored) {\n      fp.attach();\n      showTutorial(\"Timeline restored — press B to return to orbit.\", 0);\n    } else if (failed) {\n      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run\n    } else {\n      fp.attach();\n      showTutorial(\"The corruption never stopped. Find the last good checkpoint.\", 6000);\n    }\n  }\n  function exit() {\n    active = false;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n    closeClue();\n    fp.unlock();\n    fp.detach();\n    canvas.removeEventListener(\"click\", onCanvasClick);\n    window.removeEventListener(\"keydown\", onKeyDown);\n    setPrompt(null);\n    hideControls();\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    clueCard?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    l2Hud?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    fpShared?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    crosshair?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function resize() {\n    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n  }\n\n  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };\n}\n"
}

226	  // How hard the sky is panicking right now: 0 = calm lavender, 1 = full crimson throb.
227	  function panicFactor() {
228	    if (failed) return 1;                         // hold the dread behind the fail screen
229	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
230	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME; // 0 at PANIC_TIME → 1 at 0:00
231	    p *= p;                                        // eased: barely there early, harsh late
232	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {                   // throb once it's critical
233	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
234	      p += 0.14 * throb;

236	    return Math.min(1, p);

445	    if (reviewMode && listShown) {

447	      termInput.textContent = "entire checkpoint list";

458	    if (step.type === "confirm") {

470	    }

479	  }

499	  }
