```
211	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
212	  }
213	  function hideActionBar() { actionBar.classList.add("hidden"); }
214	
215	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
216	  function fmtTime(s) {
217	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
218	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
219	  }
220	  function updateClock() {
221	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
222	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
223	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
224	  }
225	
226	  // How hard the sky is panicking right now: 0 = calm lavender, 1 = full crimson throb.
227	  function panicFactor() {
228	    if (failed) return 1;                         // hold the dread behind the fail screen
229	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
230	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME; // 0 at PANIC_TIME → 1 at 0:00
231	    p *= p;                                        // eased: barely there early, harsh late
232	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {                   // throb once it's critical
233	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
234	      p += 0.14 * throb;
235	    }
236	    return Math.min(1, p);
237	  }
238	  function applyPanicSky() {
239	    const p = panicFactor();
240	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
241	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
242	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
243	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
244	  }
245	  function failLevel() {
246	    if (failed) return;
247	    failed = true;
248	    timerRunning = false;
249	    closeTerminal(false);
250	    fp.detach();                  // freeze the player behind the fail screen
251	    // Un-banked memories always melt; if they ran out the clock on the final
252	    // `entire checkpoint list` (all banked, not yet reviewed), the whole run melts.
253	    for (const a of artifacts) {
254	      if (reviewMode || a.state !== "checkpointed") { a.melting = true; a.meltT = MELT_DUR; }
255	    }
256	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
257	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
258	    levelFail?.classList.remove("hidden");
259	  }
260	  function resetLevel() {
261	    failed = false;
262	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
263	    if (active) fp.attach();      // movement resumes for the new run
264	    for (const a of artifacts) {
265	      if (a.ice) { a.anchor.remove(a.ice); a.ice = null; }
266	      if (a.idSprite) { a.anchor.remove(a.idSprite); a.idSprite = null; }
267	      if (a.core) { a.anchor.remove(a.core); a.core = null; }
268	      a.state = "dormant";
269	      a.id = null;
270	      a.melting = false; a.meltT = 0;
271	      a.model.position.y = 1.4;
272	      setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
273	    }
274	    reviewMode = false;
275	    listShown = false;
276	    dismissedMemory = null;
277	    shipMeter?.classList.remove("is-full");
278	    setPower();
279	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
280	    timerRunning = true;
281	    updateClock();
282	    showTutorial("New attempt — bank all three memories before the clock hits zero.", 4500);
283	  }
284	
285	  // Dev-only Level 2 entry point: pretend Level 1 has already been completed so
286	  // the next level can be built/tested without replaying the timed tutorial.
287	  function seedLevel2DevState() {
288	    started = true;
289	    failed = false;
290	    timerRunning = false;
291	    reviewMode = false;
292	    listShown = true;
293	    dismissedMemory = null;
294	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
295	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
296	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
297	
298	    artifacts.forEach((a, idx) => {
299	      a.state = "checkpointed";
300	      a.id = DEV_LEVEL2_CHECKPOINTS[idx % DEV_LEVEL2_CHECKPOINTS.length];
301	      a.melting = false;
302	      a.meltT = 0;
303	      a.model.position.y = 1.4;
304	      setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
305	
306	      if (!a.ice) {
307	        const ice = makeIceBlock();
308	        ice.position.y = a.model.position.y + 0.6;
309	        a.iceY = ice.position.y;
310	        a.anchor.add(ice);
311	        a.ice = ice;
312	      }
313	      a.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
314	      a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);
315	      a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
316	
317	      if (!a.core) {
318	        const core = new THREE.PointLight(0x8fe3ff, 6, 26, 2);
319	        core.position.copy(a.ice.position);
320	        a.anchor.add(core);
321	        a.core = core;
322	      }
323	      if (!a.idSprite) {
324	        const spr = makeIdSprite(a.id);
325	        spr.position.y = a.iceY + 5;
326	        a.anchor.add(spr);
327	        a.idSprite = spr;
328	      }
329	
330	      a.beam.visible = false;
331	      a.glow.visible = false;
332	    });
333	
334	    shipMeter?.classList.add("is-full");
335	    setPower();
336	    updateClock();
337	    applyPanicSky();
338	  }
339	
340	  // Landing briefing — the level is frozen (no clock, no movement) until START.
341	  function showBriefing() {
342	    timerRunning = false;
343	    fp.detach();
344	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
345	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
346	  }
347	  function startLevel() {
348	    if (started) return;
349	    started = true;
350	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
351	    fp.attach();
352	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
353	    timerRunning = true;
354	    updateClock();
355	    showTutorial("Memories are surfacing — the clock's short. Bank all three before 0:00!", 6500);
356	  }
357	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
358	
359	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
360	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
361	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
362	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
363	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
364	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
365	  }
366	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
367	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
368	    taught.add(key);
369	    showTutorial(text, ms);
370	  }
371	
372	  function setPower() {
373	    const pct = Math.round((checkpointedCount() / artifacts.length) * 100);
374	    // Vertical gauge — fills bottom-up and shifts color as memory is restored.
375	    if (shipMeterFill) shipMeterFill.style.height = pct + "%";
376	    if (shipMeterPct) shipMeterPct.textContent = pct + "%";
377	    if (shipMeter) {
378	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-low", pct > 0 && pct <= 34);
379	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-mid", pct > 34 && pct < 100);
380	      shipMeter.classList.toggle("lvl-high", pct >= 100);
381	    }
382	  }
383	  function showCard(a, shipPct) {
384	    if (!ckptCard) return;
385	    ccId.textContent = a.id;
386	    ccSummary.textContent = `Recovered: ${a.fragment.title}`;
387	    ccAttrFill.style.width = shipPct + "%";
388	    ccAttrText.textContent = `ship ${shipPct}% · you ${100 - shipPct}%`;
389	    ckptCard.classList.remove("hidden");
390	    clearTimeout(cardTimer);
391	    cardTimer = setTimeout(() => ckptCard.classList.add("hidden"), 3800);
392	  }
393	
394	  function setControls(locked) {
395	    if (!controlsEl) return;
396	    if (locked === controlsLocked && !controlsEl.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
397	    controlsLocked = locked;
398	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
399	      <span class="control-item">
400	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
401	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
402	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
403	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
404	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
405	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
406	        </span>
407	      </span>
408	      ${locked ? `
409	        <span class="control-item">
410	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
411	          <span class="mouse-hint">
412	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
413	            <span>move mouse</span>
414	          </span>
415	        </span>
416	      ` : ""}
417	      <span class="control-item">
418	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
419	        <span class="key">B</span>
420	      </span>
421	    `;
422	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
423	  }
424	  function hideControls() {
425	    controlsLocked = null;
426	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
427	  }
428	
429	  function setModelOpacity(model, o) {
430	    model.traverse((n) => {
431	      if (n.material) { n.material.transparent = o < 1; n.material.opacity = o; }
432	    });
433	  }
434	
435	  // ---------- terminal ----------
436	  function currentStep() {
437	    if (reviewMode) return REVIEW_STEP;
438	    if (!termTarget) return null;
439	    return STEPS[termTarget.state] || null;
440	  }
441	  function renderTerminal() {
442	    const step = currentStep();
443	    if (!step) return;
444	
445	    if (reviewMode && listShown) {
446	      termHint.textContent = "# entire checkpoint list";
447	      termInput.textContent = "entire checkpoint list";
448	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
449	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = "";
450	      termList?.classList.remove("hidden");
451	      return;
452	    }
453	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
454	
455	    // The hint is just the "why"; the actionable command/keys live in the CTA
456	    // below the input line, where they're the most prominent thing on screen.
457	    termHint.textContent = step.hint ? `# ${step.hint}` : "";
458	    if (step.type === "confirm") {
459	      termInput.textContent = `${step.question}  [y/n]`;
460	      termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
461	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
462	        `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
463	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd><span class="cta-note">link checkpoint</span>` +
464	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-no">N</kbd><span class="cta-note">skip</span>`;
465	    } else {
466	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
467	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
468	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
469	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">${step.cmd}</span>`;
470	    }
471	  }
472	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
473	    if (!termMsg) return;
474	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
475	    termMsg.textContent = text;
476	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
477	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
478	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 2400);
479	  }
480	  function openTerminal(a) {
481	    if (terminalOpen) return;
482	    terminalOpen = true;
483	    termTarget = a;
484	    buffer = "";
485	    fp.detach();                      // freeze movement; keystrokes feed the terminal
486	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
487	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
488	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
489	    renderTerminal();
490	  }
491	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
492	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
493	    if (dismiss && termTarget) dismissedMemory = termTarget;
494	    terminalOpen = false;
495	    termTarget = null;
496	    buffer = "";
497	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
498	    if (active) fp.attach();          // resume walking (addEventListener dedupes)
499	  }
500	  function submitCommand() {
501	    const step = currentStep();
502	    if (!step || step.type !== "command" || listShown) return;
503	    if (cmdMatches(buffer, step.accept)) {
504	      buffer = "";
505	      if (reviewMode) { showCheckpointList(); return; }
506	      runStep(termTarget);            // dormant → stage, recovered → commit
507	      flashTerminal("✓ ok", true);
508	      renderTerminal();
509	    } else {
510	      flashTerminal(`command not recognized — try:  ${step.cmd}`, false);
511	      buffer = "";
512	      renderTerminal();
513	    }
514	  }
515	  function showCheckpointList() {
516	    listShown = true;
517	    timerRunning = false;            // the run is complete — NOW the clock stops
518	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
519	    if (termList) {
520	      termList.innerHTML = artifacts.map((a) =>
521	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id">` +
522	        `<span class="tl-key">Entire-Checkpoint:</span> ${a.id}</span>` +
523	        `<span class="tl-title">recovered: ${a.fragment.title}</span></div>`
524	      ).join("");
525	    }
526	    flashTerminal(`${artifacts.length} checkpoints linked · ship memory restored`, true);
527	    showTutorial("Memory restored — the ship knows the way home. Press Esc to close the terminal, then B to return to orbit.", 0);
528	    renderTerminal();
529	    onComplete?.();                  // Level 1 cleared — unlocks Level 2 in orbit
530	  }
531	
532	  // ---------- state transitions ----------
533	  function runStep(a) {
534	    if (a.state === "dormant") stage(a);
535	    else if (a.state === "recovered") commit(a);
536	  }
537	  function stage(a) {
538	    a.state = "recovered";
539	    teachOnce("staged",
540	      "Recovered and staged. Run `git commit` to freeze it — the clock is ticking.");
541	  }
542	  function commit(a) {
543	    a.state = "frozen";
544	    setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
545	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
546	    ice.position.y = a.model.position.y + 0.6;
547	    a.iceY = ice.position.y;
548	    a.anchor.add(ice);
549	    a.ice = ice;
550	    teachOnce("committed",
551	      "Committed and frozen — locked in. Now Entire offers to link a checkpoint: press y to capture the session behind this commit.");
552	  }
553	  function confirmCheckpoint(yes) {
554	    const a = termTarget;
555	    if (!a || a.state !== "frozen") return;
556	    if (!yes) {
557	      flashTerminal("declined — without a checkpoint the ship can't track this commit. re-approach to link it.", false);
558	      closeTerminal(true);
559	      return;
560	    }
561	    checkpoint(a);
562	    if (allLinked()) {
563	      reviewMode = true;             // clock KEEPS running — the list is the finish line
564	      shipMeter?.classList.add("is-full");
565	      showTutorial("All banked! Now type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00 — hurry!", 0);
566	      renderTerminal();              // stay open → review step
567	    } else {
568	      closeTerminal(false);
569	    }
570	  }
571	  function checkpoint(a) {
572	    a.state = "checkpointed";
573	    a.id = genCheckpointId();
574	    const shipPct = 62 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 23); // 62–84%
575	
576	    if (a.ice) {
577	      a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);
578	      a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
579	      const core = new THREE.PointLight(0x8fe3ff, 6, 26, 2);
580	      core.position.copy(a.ice.position);
581	      a.anchor.add(core);
582	      a.core = core;
583	    }
584	    const spr = makeIdSprite(a.id);
585	    spr.position.y = a.iceY + 5;
586	    a.anchor.add(spr);
587	    a.idSprite = spr;
588	
589	    showCard(a, shipPct);
590	    setPower();
591	    teachOnce("checkpointed",
592	      "Checkpoint linked — Entire stamps it onto the commit via an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer, and the ship remembers a little more.");
593	  }
594	
595	  // ---------- input ----------
596	  function onCanvasClick() {
597	    if (!started) return;                 // briefing up — ignore world clicks
598	    if (active && !fp.isLocked) fp.lock();
599	  }
600	  function onKeyDown(e) {
601	    if (!active) return;
602	
603	    // Landing briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
604	    if (!started) {
605	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
606	      return;
607	    }
608	
609	    // The run failed — only R (retry) does anything.
610	    if (failed) {
611	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
612	      return;
613	    }
614	
615	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
616	    if (terminalOpen) {
617	      if (e.code === "Escape") {
618	        // No bailing on the final review — the clock is still ticking.
619	        if (reviewMode && !listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
620	        closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return;
621	      }
622	      if (reviewMode && listShown) { e.preventDefault(); return; } // only Esc closes the list
623	
624	      const step = currentStep();
625	      if (step?.type === "confirm") {
626	        if (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y") { confirmCheckpoint(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
627	        if (e.key === "n" || e.key === "N") { confirmCheckpoint(false); e.preventDefault(); return; }
628	        e.preventDefault();
629	        return;
630	      }
631	      // command mode
632	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
633	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
634	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
635	        buffer += e.key;
636	        renderTerminal();
637	        e.preventDefault();
638	      }
639	      return;
640	    }
641	
642	    // Walking around (terminal closed).
643	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
644	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
645	  }
646	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
647	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
648	
649	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
650	  function refreshHud() {
651	    if (!started || terminalOpen) {     // briefing up or terminal open → no walk HUD
652	      setPrompt(null);
653	      hideControls();
654	      hideActionBar();
655	      return;
656	    }
657	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
658	    hideActionBar();
659	    if (devLevel2) {
660	      setPrompt(fp.isLocked ? "Level 2 dev start — press B to return to orbit" : "Click to look around");
661	      return;
662	    }
663	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
664	    if (listShown) { setPrompt("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit"); return; }
665	    setPrompt("Find the surfacing memory");
666	  }
667	
668	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
669	  function update(dt, t) {
670	    fp.update(dt);
671	
672	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
673	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed) {
674	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
675	      updateClock();
676	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
677	    }
678	    applyPanicSky();   // the sky reacts to the clock every frame
679	
680	    // Nearest memory within reach (ignoring height).
681	    let nearest = null, nd = Infinity;
682	    for (const a of artifacts) {
683	      const dx = camera.position.x - a.anchor.position.x;
684	      const dz = camera.position.z - a.anchor.position.z;
685	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
686	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; nearest = a; }
687	    }
688	    target = nd <= INTERACT_DIST ? nearest : null;
689	
690	    // Auto open/close the ship's terminal as you reach a memory.
691	    // (In review mode the terminal is driven manually, so leave it alone.)
692	    if (active && started && !reviewMode && !failed && !devLevel2) {
693	      if (dismissedMemory && target !== dismissedMemory) dismissedMemory = null;
694	      const canOpen = target && target.state !== "checkpointed" && target !== dismissedMemory;
695	      if (canOpen && !terminalOpen) openTerminal(target);
696	      else if (!canOpen && terminalOpen) closeTerminal(false);
697	    }
698	
699	    for (const a of artifacts) {
700	      // Melting away after a time-out — sink and fade, then disappear.
701	      if (a.melting) {
702	        a.meltT = Math.max(0, a.meltT - dt);
703	        const k = a.meltT / MELT_DUR;
704	        setModelOpacity(a.model, k);
705	        a.model.position.y -= dt * 2.4;
706	        if (a.ice) a.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * k;
707	        a.beam.visible = false;
708	        a.glow.visible = false;
709	        continue;
710	      }
711	
712	      const encased = a.state === "frozen" || a.state === "checkpointed";
713	
714	      // Idle spin/bob until the memory is encased in ice.
715	      if (!encased) {
716	        a.model.rotation.y += dt * 0.6;
717	        a.model.position.y = 1.4 + Math.sin((t + a.bobOff) * 1.5) * 0.4;
718	        setModelOpacity(a.model, 1);
719	      }
720	
721	      // Beam + glow: bright while findable, gone once frozen.
722	      const lit = a.state === "dormant" || a.state === "recovered";
723	      let o = 0;
724	      if (a.state === "dormant") o = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.12;
725	      else if (a.state === "recovered") o = 0.22;
726	      a.beam.material.opacity = o;
727	      a.beam.visible = o > 0.01;
728	      a.glow.visible = lit;
729	    }
730	
731	    if (terminalOpen) renderTerminal(); // reflect fade-driven state changes
732	    if (active) refreshHud();
733	  }
734	
735	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
736	  function enter() {
737	    active = true;
738	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
739	    camera.lookAt(0, terrain.heightAt(0, 0) + 6, 0);
740	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
741	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
742	    islandHud?.classList.remove("hidden");
743	    islandHud?.classList.toggle("is-level2-dev", devLevel2);
744	    devLevelBadge?.classList.toggle("hidden", !devLevel2);
745	    fpShared?.classList.remove("hidden");
746	    setControls(false);
747	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
748	    setPower();
749	    if (devLevel2) {
750	      seedLevel2DevState();
751	      fp.attach();
752	      showTutorial("Level 2 dev mode — Level 1 checkpoints are preloaded.", 0);
753	    } else if (!started) {          // fresh landing — read the briefing, then START
754	      showBriefing();
755	    } else if (listShown) {         // truly done — the list has been run
756	      fp.attach();
757	      timerRunning = false;
758	      showTutorial("Memory restored — press B to return to orbit.", 0);
759	    } else if (failed) {
760	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
761	    } else {
762	      fp.attach();
763	      timerRunning = true;          // resume the level clock
764	      if (reviewMode) {             // resume the timed final review
765	        openTerminal(artifacts[0]); // termTarget unused in review; reopens the prompt
766	        showTutorial("Type `entire checkpoint list` before the clock hits 0:00!", 0);
767	      } else {
768	        showTutorial("Memories are surfacing — the clock's short. Bank all three before 0:00!", 6500);
769	      }
770	    }
771	    updateClock();
772	    applyPanicSky();   // start on the right sky (calm unless we resumed low on time)
773	  }
774	  function exit() {
775	    active = false;
776	    timerRunning = false;           // pause the clock while in orbit
777	    closeTerminal(false);
778	    fp.unlock();
779	    fp.detach();
780	    canvas.removeEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
781	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
782	    setPrompt(null);
783	    hideControls();
784	    hideActionBar();
785	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
786	    ckptCard?.classList.add("hidden");
787	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
788	    levelFail?.classList.add("hidden");
789	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
790	    islandHud?.classList.add("hidden");
791	    islandHud?.classList.remove("is-level2-dev");
792	    devLevelBadge?.classList.add("hidden");
793	    fpShared?.classList.add("hidden");
794	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
795	  }
796	  function resize() {
797	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
798	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
799	  }
800	
801	  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };
802	}
803
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	
3	// Shared props for the memory levels: the findable light beam, the ice block a
4	// commit freezes a memory into, and the floating `Entire-Checkpoint: <id>`
5	// trailer sprite. Used by Level 1 (islandView) and Level 2 (corruptionView).
6	
7	export function makeBeamTexture() {
8	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
9	  c.width = 8; c.height = 128;
10	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
11	  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);
12	  g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,210,122,0)");
13	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,210,122,0.9)");
14	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
15	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 128);
16	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
17	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
18	  return t;
19	}
20	
21	// Translucent crystal that encases a memory once it's committed.
22	export function makeIceBlock() {
23	  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.4, 0);
24	  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
25	    color: 0xbfe9ff,
26	    metalness: 0,
27	    roughness: 0.06,
28	    transmission: 0.5,
29	    thickness: 2.6,
30	    ior: 1.3,
31	    transparent: true,
32	    opacity: 0.62,
33	    emissive: 0x0a2230,
34	    emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
35	    flatShading: true,
36	  });
37	  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
38	}
39	
40	// Floating label above a frozen block — the commit trailer Entire stamps on:
41	//   "Entire-Checkpoint: <12-char hash>" (dim key + bright hash).
42	export function makeIdSprite(id) {
43	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
44	  c.width = 768; c.height = 64;
45	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
46	  ctx.font = "bold 28px ui-monospace, monospace";
47	  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
48	  ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(111,227,255,0.9)";
49	  ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
50	
51	  const key = "Entire-Checkpoint: ";
52	  const keyW = ctx.measureText(key).width;
53	  const idW = ctx.measureText(id).width;
54	  let x = (c.width - (keyW + idW)) / 2;       // center the whole trailer
55	  ctx.textAlign = "left";
56	  ctx.fillStyle = "#8fc4dc";                    // dim trailer key
57	  ctx.fillText(key, x, 34);
58	  ctx.fillStyle = "#dff3ff";                    // bright hash
59	  ctx.fillText(id, x + keyW, 34);
60	
61	  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
62	  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
63	  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
64	    map: tex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
65	  }));
66	  spr.scale.set(30, 2.5, 1);                    // 12:1 canvas → keep text height
67	  return spr;
68	}
69	
70	// Entire checkpoints get a 12-character hex id (e.g. 711044b1fe29).
71	export function genCheckpointId() {
72	  const hex = "0123456789abcdef";
73	  let s = "";
74	  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) s += hex[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
75	  return s;
76	}
77
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";

3	// Radial-gradient sprite used as the attention halo under each debris item.
4	function makeHaloTexture(color = "#6fe3ff") {
5	  const s = 128;
6	  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
7	  c.width = c.height = s;
8	  const ctx = c.getContext("2d");
9	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(s / 2, s / 2, 0, s / 2, s / 2, s / 2);
10	  g.addColorStop(0, color);
11	  g.addColorStop(0.25, color);
12	  g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
13	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
14	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, s, s);
15	  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
16	  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
17	  return t;
18	}
19	
20	const HALO_TEX = { value: null };
21	function haloTexture() {
22	  if (!HALO_TEX.value) HALO_TEX.value = makeHaloTexture();
23	  return HALO_TEX.value;
24	}
25	
26	// ---- tiny model builders for each kind of debris ----

28	  const g = new THREE.Group();
29	  const glass = new THREE.Mesh(
30	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.06, 24, 24),
31	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x223344, metalness: 0.3, roughness: 0.15 })
32	  );
33	  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
34	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.06, 0.015, 12, 24),
35	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xdfe6ee, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.4 })
36	  );
37	  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;

40	  return g;

116	    title: "Torn Logbook Page",
117	    body: "Coordinates, half-erased by rain. Beneath the smudged ink a single checkpoint marker bleeds through — as if someone wanted a way back to this exact moment.",
118	    hint: "entire checkpoint",
119	  },
120	  {
121	    kind: "panel",
122	    lat: 52, lon: 18,
123	    title: "Cracked Nav Panel",
124	    body: "Diagnostics flatlined mid-write. The activity trail scrolls up the shattered glass and then simply… stops. The last legible line is a timestamp from the night of the crash.",
125	    hint: "entire activity",
126	  },
127	  {
128	    kind: "beacon",
129	    lat: -40, lon: -120,
130	    title: "Signal Beacon",
131	    body: "Still pulsing, somehow. It's been broadcasting the same dispatch into the dark for days — a summary of work no one is left to read.",
132	    hint: "entire dispatch",
133	  },
134	];
135	
136	// Convert lat/lon (degrees) to a point on a sphere of the given radius.
137	function latLonToVec3(lat, lon, radius) {
138	  const phi = (90 - lat) * (Math.PI / 180);
139	  const theta = (lon + 180) * (Math.PI / 180);
140	  return new THREE.Vector3(
141	    -radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
142	    radius * Math.cos(phi),
143	    radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta)
144	  );
145	}
146	
147	/**
148	 * Build debris anchors on the planet surface. Returns the group (to add to the
149	 * planet so they rotate together) and the list of pickable meshes for
150	 * raycasting, each carrying its fragment data in userData.
151	 */
152	export function createDebris(planetRadius) {
153	  const group = new THREE.Group();
154	  const pickables = [];
155	
156	  for (const frag of FRAGMENTS) {
157	    const pos = latLonToVec3(frag.lat, frag.lon, planetRadius);
158	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
159	    anchor.position.copy(pos);
160	    // Orient so local +Y points away from the planet centre.
161	    anchor.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(
162	      new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
163	      pos.clone().normalize()
164	    );
165	
166	    const model = BUILDERS[frag.kind]();
167	    model.position.y = 0.02;
168	
169	    // Halo to make it findable; doubles as the click target (big & flat).
170	    const halo = new THREE.Mesh(
171	      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.34, 0.34),
172	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
173	        map: haloTexture(),
174	        transparent: true,
175	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
176	        depthWrite: false,
177	        opacity: 0.9,
178	      })
179	    );
180	    halo.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
181	    halo.position.y = 0.005;
182	
183	    anchor.add(halo, model);
184	    anchor.userData = { fragment: frag, halo, model, baseScale: 1 };
185	    group.add(anchor);
186	    pickables.push(anchor);
187	  }
188	
189	  return { group, pickables };
190	}
191	
```

```
586	  left: 32px;
587	  display: flex;
588	  align-items: center;
589	  gap: 11px;
590	  padding: 10px 16px;
591	  background: rgba(14, 5, 16, 0.78);
592	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.4);
593	  border-radius: 9px;
594	  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
595	}
596	.cm-label {
597	  font-size: 10.5px;
598	  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
599	  font-weight: 700;
600	  color: #ff8ad8;
601	  opacity: 0.95;
602	}
603	.cm-track {
604	  position: relative;
605	  width: 130px;
606	  height: 10px;
607	  border-radius: 6px;
608	  background: rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.14);
609	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.22);
610	  overflow: hidden;
611	}
612	#corrupt-fill {
613	  position: absolute;
614	  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
615	  width: 0%;
616	  border-radius: 6px;
617	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #7a1257, #ff2bd6);
618	  box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.7);
619	  transition: width 0.4s linear;
620	}
621	#corrupt-pct {
622	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
623	  font-size: 16px;
624	  font-weight: 700;
625	  min-width: 44px;
626	  text-align: right;
627	  color: var(--ink);
628	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
629	}
630	#corrupt-meter.is-high {
631	  border-color: rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.85);
632	  animation: cd-pulse 1s ease-in-out infinite;
633	}
634	
635	/* Clue tracker — words gathered for the search */
636	#clue-tracker {
637	  position: absolute;
638	  top: 134px;
639	  left: 32px;
640	  display: flex;
641	  align-items: center;
642	  gap: 11px;
643	  padding: 8px 16px;
644	  background: rgba(6, 11, 22, 0.7);
645	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.32);
646	  border-radius: 9px;
647	  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
648	}
649	#clue-tracker .cm-label { color: var(--accent-warm); }
650	#clue-count {
651	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
652	  font-size: 15px;
653	  font-weight: 700;
654	  color: var(--ink);
655	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
656	}
657	
658	/* Clue card — what a frozen memory remembers */
659	#clue-card {
660	  position: absolute;
661	  top: 50%;
662	  left: 50%;
663	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.96);
664	  width: min(380px, 88vw);
665	  padding: 20px 22px;
666	  background: rgba(10, 8, 20, 0.92);
667	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.45);
668	  border-radius: 10px;
669	  box-shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.6), 0 0 30px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.16);
670	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
671	  opacity: 0;
672	  transition: opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
673	}
674	#clue-card:not(.hidden) {
675	  opacity: 1;
676	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1);
677	}
678	.qc-head {
679	  font-size: 11px;
680	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
681	  color: var(--accent-warm);
682	}
683	.qc-title {
684	  margin: 10px 0 8px;
685	  font-size: 16px;
686	  font-weight: 700;
687	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
688	}
689	.qc-body {
690	  font-size: 13px;
691	  line-height: 1.6;
692	  color: var(--ink);
693	  opacity: 0.85;
694	  margin-bottom: 14px;
695	}
696	.qc-keyrow {
697	  padding-top: 11px;
698	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.2);
699	  font-size: 11.5px;
700	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.55);
701	}
702	.qc-keyrow span {
703	  color: var(--accent-warm);
704	  font-weight: 700;
705	}
706	.qc-foot {
707	  margin-top: 12px;
708	  font-size: 11px;
709	  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
710	  color: var(--ink);
711	  opacity: 0.6;
712	}
713	
714	/* The good rewind landed */
715	#l2-win {
716	  position: absolute;
717	  top: 50%;
718	  left: 50%;
719	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
720	  width: min(440px, 88vw);
721	  padding: 28px 30px;
722	  text-align: center;
723	  background: rgba(8, 18, 16, 0.92);
724	  border: 1px solid rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.5);
725	  border-radius: 12px;
726	  box-shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.6), 0 0 40px rgba(92, 255, 176, 0.2);
727	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
728	}
729	.lw-title {
730	  font-size: 20px;
731	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
732	  font-weight: 700;
733	  color: #7cffb0;
734	  margin-bottom: 10px;
735	}
736	.lw-sub {
737	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
738	  font-size: 12px;
739	  line-height: 1.5;
740	  color: var(--accent);
741	  margin-bottom: 16px;
742	}
743	.lw-reveal {
744	  font-size: 12.5px;
745	  line-height: 1.6;
746	  color: var(--ink);
747	  opacity: 0.85;
748	  margin-bottom: 18px;
749	}
750	.lw-reveal code {
751	  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;
752	  font-size: 11.5px;
753	  padding: 1px 5px;
754	  border-radius: 4px;
755	  background: rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.1);
756	  color: var(--accent);
757	}
758	
759	/* Magenta flash when a rewind lands on a tainted checkpoint */
760	#glitch-flash {
761	  position: fixed;
762	  inset: 0;
763	  background: #ff2bd6;
764	  opacity: 0;
765	  pointer-events: none;
766	  z-index: 35;
767	}
768	#glitch-flash.show {
769	  animation: glitch-flash 0.5s steps(3, end) both;
770	}
771	@keyframes glitch-flash {
772	  0% { opacity: 0.45; }
773	  40% { opacity: 0.12; }
774	  70% { opacity: 0.3; }
775	  100% { opacity: 0; }
776	}
777	
778	/* Teaching line */
779	#tutorial {
780	  position: absolute;
781	  top: 132px;
782	  left: 50%;
783	  transform: translateX(-50%);
784	  max-width: min(560px, calc(100vw - 48px));
785	  padding: 10px 18px;
786	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.62);
787	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.32);
788	  border-radius: 6px;
789	  font-size: 12.5px;
790	  line-height: 1.5;
791	  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
792	  text-align: center;
793	  color: var(--ink);
794	  opacity: 0;
795	  transition: opacity 0.4s ease;
796	}
797	#tutorial:not(.hidden) { opacity: 1; }
798	
799	/* Action progress bar (recover fill / fade countdown) */
800	#action-bar {
801	  position: absolute;
802	  bottom: 176px;
803	  left: 50%;
804	  transform: translateX(-50%);
805	  width: 220px;
806	  height: 6px;
807	  border-radius: 4px;
808	  background: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.12);
809	  overflow: hidden;
810	}
811	
812	#action-bar-fill {
813	  display: block;
814	  height: 100%;
815	  width: 0%;
816	  border-radius: 4px;
817	}
818	
819	#action-bar.is-recover #action-bar-fill {
820	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3aa6c8, var(--accent));
821	  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.6);
822	}
823	
824	#action-bar.is-fade #action-bar-fill {
825	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ff7a5c, var(--accent-warm));
826	  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.6);
827	}
828	
829	/* Checkpoint record card (the middle-ground "anatomy") */
830	#ckpt-card {
831	  position: absolute;
832	  top: 50%;
833	  left: 50%;
834	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.96);
835	  width: min(340px, 86vw);
836	  padding: 18px 20px;
837	  background: rgba(8, 16, 28, 0.9);
838	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.4);
839	  border-radius: 10px;
840	  box-shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.6), 0 0 30px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.18);
841	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
842	  opacity: 0;
843	  transition: opacity 0.35s ease, transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
844	}
845	
846	#ckpt-card:not(.hidden) {
847	  opacity: 1;
848	  transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1);
849	}
850	
851	.cc-head {
852	  font-size: 11px;
853	  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
854	  color: var(--accent);
855	}
856	#cc-id { color: var(--ink); }
857	
858	.cc-summary {
859	  margin: 12px 0 14px;
860	  font-size: 15px;
861	  line-height: 1.4;
862	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
863	}
864	
865	.cc-attr {
866	  display: flex;
867	  align-items: center;
868	  gap: 10px;
869	}
870	
871	.cc-attr-bar {
872	  position: relative;
873	  flex: 1;
874	  height: 6px;
875	  border-radius: 4px;
876	  background: rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.22);
877	  overflow: hidden;
878	}
879	
880	#cc-attr-fill {
881	  position: absolute;
882	  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
883	  width: 70%;
884	  background: var(--accent);
885	}
886	
887	#cc-attr-text {
888	  font-size: 10.5px;
889	  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
890	  opacity: 0.75;
891	  white-space: nowrap;
892	}
893	
894	.cc-trailer {
895	  margin-top: 13px;
896	  padding-top: 11px;
897	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
898	  font-size: 11px;
899	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
900	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.5);
901	}
902	.cc-trailer #cc-id { color: var(--accent); opacity: 1; }
903	
904	.cc-foot {
905	  margin-top: 10px;
906	  font-size: 10px;
907	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
908	  text-transform: uppercase;
909	  color: var(--accent);
910	  opacity: 0.75;
911	}
912	
913	/* Ship terminal — type the real commands */
914	#terminal {
915	  position: absolute;
916	  bottom: 70px;
917	  left: 50%;
918	  transform: translateX(-50%);
919	  width: min(560px, calc(100vw - 40px));
920	  padding: 14px 16px;
921	  background: rgba(4, 10, 18, 0.88);
922	  border: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.42);
923	  border-radius: 8px;
924	  box-shadow: 0 14px 40px rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.55), 0 0 24px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.12);
925	  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
926	  text-align: left;
927	}
928	
929	#term-hint {
930	  font-size: 12.5px;
931	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
932	  color: var(--ink);
933	  opacity: 0.68;
934	  margin-bottom: 10px;
935	}
936	
937	#term-line {
938	  display: flex;
939	  align-items: center;
940	  font-size: 16px;
941	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
942	}
943	
944	.term-prompt {
945	  color: var(--accent);
946	  opacity: 0.9;
947	  margin-right: 8px;
948	}
949	
950	#term-input { color: var(--ink); white-space: pre; }
951	#term-input.is-dim { color: var(--accent-warm); opacity: 0.92; }
952	
953	/* Call-to-action: the actionable command/keys — the brightest thing here */
954	#term-cta {
955	  display: flex;
956	  align-items: center;
957	  gap: 10px;
958	  margin-top: 13px;
959	}
960	#term-cta:empty { display: none; }
961	.cta-label {
962	  font-size: 10px;
963	  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
964	  font-weight: 700;
965	  color: var(--accent);
966	  opacity: 0.9;
967	}
968	.cta-cmd {
969	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
970	  font-size: 16px;
971	  font-weight: 700;
972	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
973	  color: #04121a;
974	  padding: 5px 14px;
975	  border-radius: 7px;
976	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
977	  box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
978	}
979	.cta-key {
980	  display: inline-grid;
981	  place-items: center;
982	  min-width: 26px;
983	  height: 26px;
984	  padding: 0 7px;
985	  font-family: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace;
986	  font-size: 14px;
987	  font-weight: 700;
988	  border-radius: 6px;
989	  color: #04121a;
990	}
991	.cta-key-yes {
992	  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #6fe3ff, #9af0ff);
993	  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.45);
994	}
995	.cta-key-no {
996	  background: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.16);
997	  color: var(--ink);
998	  border: 1px solid rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.3);
999	}
1000	.cta-note {
1001	  font-size: 12.5px;
1002	  color: var(--ink);
1003	  opacity: 0.82;
1004	  margin-right: 8px;
1005	}
1006	
1007	/* `entire checkpoint list` output */
1008	#term-list:not(.hidden) {
1009	  display: flex;
1010	  flex-direction: column;
1011	  gap: 5px;
1012	  margin-top: 11px;
1013	  padding-top: 10px;
1014	  border-top: 1px solid rgba(111, 227, 255, 0.16);
1015	}
1016	.term-list-row {
1017	  display: flex;
1018	  gap: 12px;
1019	  font-size: 12.5px;
1020	  line-height: 1.3;
1021	}
1022	.tl-id { color: var(--accent); min-width: 300px; white-space: nowrap; }
1023	.tl-key { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.55; }
1024	.tl-title { color: var(--ink); opacity: 0.82; }
1025	
1026	/* Level 2 terminal output: search results, explain blocks, rewind browser.
1027	   These rows carry a time column, so the id column shrinks to fit. */
1028	.term-list-row .tl-time {
1029	  color: var(--accent-warm);
1030	  min-width: 44px;
1031	  white-space: nowrap;
1032	}
1033	.term-list-row:has(.tl-time) .tl-id { min-width: 120px; }
1034	.term-list-row .tl-sel { width: 12px; flex: none; color: #ff2bd6; }
1035	.tl-exp-key { min-width: 90px; flex: none; }
1036	.tl-anomaly { color: #ff8ad8; opacity: 0.95; }
1037	.term-list-row.is-sel {
1038	  background: rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.12);
1039	  outline: 1px solid rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.35);
1040	  border-radius: 4px;
1041	}
1042	
1043	.term-cursor {
1044	  display: inline-block;
1045	  width: 8px;
1046	  height: 16px;
1047	  background: var(--accent);
1048	  animation: term-blink 1s steps(1) infinite;
1049	}
1050	
1051	@keyframes term-blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
1052	
1053	#term-msg {
1054	  min-height: 13px;
1055	  margin-top: 9px;
1056	  font-size: 11.5px;
1057	  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
1058	  opacity: 0;
1059	  transition: opacity 0.15s ease;
1060	}
1061	#term-msg.show-ok { opacity: 0.92; color: #7cffb0; }
1062	#term-msg.show-err { opacity: 0.92; color: #ff8a73; }
1063	
1064	/* Map pin marking the landable island */
1065	#island-pin {
1066	  position: fixed;
1067	  top: 0; left: 0;
1068	  transform: translate(-50%, -100%);
1069	  padding: 0;
1070	  background: none;
1071	  border: none;
1072	  cursor: pointer;
1073	  pointer-events: auto;
1074	  z-index: 15;
1075	  transition: filter 0.2s ease, transform 0.18s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
1076	  transform-origin: bottom center;
1077	  filter:
1078	    drop-shadow(0 2px 4px var(--pin-shadow))
1079	    drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.3));
1080	}
1081	#island-pin .pin-svg { display: block; }
1082	#island-pin .pin-svg path {
1083	  fill: var(--pin-shell);
1084	  stroke: var(--pin-rim);
1085	  stroke-width: 1.35;
1086	}
1087	#island-pin .pin-svg circle {
1088	  fill: var(--pin-core);
1089	}
1090	/* Hover (or keyboard focus, or synced from the 3D region) lifts the pin. */
1091	#island-pin:hover,
1092	#island-pin:focus-visible,
1093	#island-pin.is-hot {
1094	  transform: translate(-50%, -100%) scale(1.12);
1095	  filter:
1096	    drop-shadow(0 3px 5px var(--pin-shadow))
1097	    drop-shadow(0 0 12px rgba(255, 184, 107, 0.62));
1098	  outline: none;
1099	}
1100	
1101	/* After Level 1, the island's signal corrupts — the pin glitches magenta. */
1102	#island-pin.is-corrupted .pin-svg circle { fill: #ff2bd6; }
1103	#island-pin.is-corrupted .pin-svg path { stroke: rgba(255, 138, 216, 0.9); }
1104	#island-pin.is-corrupted {
1105	  filter:
1106	    drop-shadow(0 2px 4px var(--pin-shadow))
1107	    drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.7));
1108	  animation: pin-glitch 2.2s steps(2, end) infinite;
1109	}
1110	@keyframes pin-glitch {
1111	  0%, 88% { transform: translate(-50%, -100%); }
1112	  90% { transform: translate(calc(-50% - 2px), -100%) skewX(6deg); }
1113	  94% { transform: translate(calc(-50% + 2px), -100%) skewX(-5deg); }
1114	  97%, 100% { transform: translate(-50%, -100%); }
1115	}
1116	
1117	/* Crossfade overlay between views */
1118	#fade {
1119	  position: fixed;
1120	  inset: 0;
1121	  background: #07050f;
1122	  opacity: 0;
1123	  pointer-events: none;
1124	  z-index: 40;
1125	  transition: opacity 0.65s ease;
1126	}
1127	#fade.show { opacity: 1; }
1128	
1129	/* First-person island HUD */
1130	#crosshair {
1131	  position: absolute;
1132	  top: 50%;
1133	  left: 50%;
1134	  width: 10px;
1135	  height: 10px;
1136	  margin: -5px 0 0 -5px;
1137	  border: 1.5px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.7);
1138	  border-radius: 50%;
1139	  box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.6);
1140	}
1141	
1142	#fp-prompt {
1143	  position: absolute;
1144	  bottom: 138px;
1145	  left: 50%;
1146	  transform: translateX(-50%);
1147	  width: max-content;
1148	  max-width: calc(100vw - 36px);
1149	  padding: 12px 22px;
1150	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.7);
1151	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);
1152	  border-radius: 6px;
1153	  font-size: 14px;
1154	  line-height: 1.45;
1155	  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
1156	  color: var(--ink);
1157	  text-align: center;
1158	  text-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(255, 210, 122, 0.4);
1159	}
1160	
1161	#fp-controls {
1162	  position: absolute;
1163	  right: 28px;
1164	  bottom: 28px;
1165	  max-width: min(720px, calc(100vw - 56px));
1166	  padding: 9px 12px;
1167	  background: rgba(8, 14, 26, 0.54);
1168	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.18);
1169	  border-radius: 6px;
1170	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.72);
1171	  font-size: 10px;
1172	  line-height: 1;
1173	  letter-spacing: 0;
1174	}
1175	
1176	#fp-controls:not(.hidden) {
1177	  display: flex;
1178	  flex-wrap: wrap;
1179	  align-items: center;
1180	  justify-content: flex-end;
1181	  gap: 10px 14px;
1182	}
1183	
1184	.control-item {
1185	  display: inline-flex;
1186	  align-items: center;
1187	  gap: 7px;
1188	  min-height: 28px;
1189	  white-space: nowrap;
1190	}
1191	
1192	.control-label {
1193	  color: rgba(232, 244, 255, 0.68);
1194	  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
1195	}
1196	
1197	.key {
1198	  display: inline-flex;
1199	  align-items: center;
1200	  justify-content: center;
1201	  min-width: 20px;
1202	  height: 20px;
1203	  padding: 0 5px;
1204	  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.26);
1205	  border-radius: 4px;
1206	  background: rgba(255, 244, 232, 0.08);
1207	  color: var(--ink);
1208	  font-size: 10px;
1209	  font-weight: 600;
1210	  line-height: 1;
1211	  box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(2, 4, 10, 0.4);
1212	}
```

```
7	// LEVEL 2 ("The Corruption") — the same island, revisited.

145	export function createCorruptionView(renderer, { onExit } = {}) {

172	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
173	
174	  // Per-vertex distance from the corruption origin + pristine colors, so the
175	  // zone can be repainted (and healed) every frame without re-noising.
176	  const geo = terrain.mesh.geometry;

375	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
376	            <span>move mouse</span>
377	          </span>
378	        </span>
379	      ` : ""}
380	      <span class="control-item">
381	        <span class="control-label">Inspect memory</span>
382	        <span class="key">E</span>
383	      </span>
384	      <span class="control-item">
385	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
386	        <span class="key">B</span>
387	      </span>
388	    `;
389	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
390	  }
391	  function hideControls() {
392	    controlsLocked = null;
393	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
394	  }
395	
396	  // ---------- corruption meter ----------
397	  function corruptionPct() {
398	    return Math.min(100, Math.round(
399	      ((radius - START_RADIUS) / (FAIL_RADIUS - START_RADIUS)) * 100
400	    ));
401	  }
402	  function updateMeter() {
403	    const pct = restored ? 0 : corruptionPct();
404	    if (corruptFill) corruptFill.style.width = pct + "%";
405	    if (corruptPct) corruptPct.textContent = pct + "%";
406	    corruptMeter?.classList.toggle("is-high", !restored && pct >= 70);
407	    if (clueCount) clueCount.textContent = `${cluesFound()} / ${artifacts.length}`;
408	  }
409	
410	  // ---------- the spreading wound ----------
411	  // Repaint terrain vertex colors around the front every frame: consumed land
412	  // collapses to void, with a flickering magenta band at the leading edge.
413	  function paintCorruption(t) {
414	    const cols = colAttr.array;
415	    const flicker = 0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 9);
416	    const reach = radius + EDGE_BAND;
417	    for (let v = 0; v < posAttr.count; v++) {
418	      const d = vertDist[v];
419	      const i = v * 3;
420	      if (d >= reach) {
421	        if (cols[i] !== baseColors[i] || cols[i + 1] !== baseColors[i + 1]) {
422	          cols[i] = baseColors[i];
423	          cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1];
424	          cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2];
425	        }
426	        continue;
427	      }
428	      if (d <= radius) {
429	        // Inside the wound — sink to void, deeper toward the origin.
430	        const k = Math.min(1, (radius - d) / 10 + 0.45);
431	        cols[i] = baseColors[i] + (C_VOID[0] - baseColors[i]) * k;
432	        cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1] + (C_VOID[1] - baseColors[i + 1]) * k;
433	        cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2] + (C_VOID[2] - baseColors[i + 2]) * k;
434	      } else {
435	        // The leading edge — a thin magenta burn.
436	        const k = (1 - (d - radius) / EDGE_BAND) * flicker;
437	        cols[i] = baseColors[i] + (C_EDGE[0] - baseColors[i]) * k;
438	        cols[i + 1] = baseColors[i + 1] + (C_EDGE[1] - baseColors[i + 1]) * k;
439	        cols[i + 2] = baseColors[i + 2] + (C_EDGE[2] - baseColors[i + 2]) * k;
440	      }
441	    }
442	    colAttr.needsUpdate = true;
443	  }
444	
445	  function updateMotes(t) {
446	    const arr = moteGeo.attributes.position.array;
447	    for (let i = 0; i < MOTES; i++) {
448	      const r = moteFrac[i] * radius;
449	      const x = ORIGIN.x + Math.cos(moteAng[i] + t * 0.05) * r;
450	      const z = ORIGIN.z + Math.sin(moteAng[i] + t * 0.05) * r;
451	      arr[i * 3] = x;
452	      arr[i * 3 + 1] = terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 1.2 +
453	        Math.sin(t * 1.6 + motePhase[i]) * 1.0 + ((t * 0.8 + motePhase[i]) % 3);
454	      arr[i * 3 + 2] = z;
455	    }
456	    moteGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
457	    motes.material.opacity = restored ? 0 : 0.45 + 0.3 * Math.sin(t * 7);
458	  }
459	
460	  // Sky drains toward the void as the corruption nears the terminal; a good
461	  // rewind lerps everything back to Level 1's calm lavender.
462	  function applySky() {
463	    if (restored || restoring) {
464	      const k = restoring ? Math.min(1, restoreT / RESTORE_DUR) : 1;
465	      scene.background.copy(SKY_SICK).lerp(SKY_HEALED, k);
466	      scene.fog.color.copy(FOG_SICK).lerp(SKY_HEALED, k);
467	      dome.material.color.copy(DOME_SICK).lerp(DOME_HEALED, k);
468	      sun.color.copy(SUN_SICK).lerp(SUN_HEALED, k);
469	      return;
470	    }
471	    let p = corruptionPct() / 100;
472	    if (failed) p = 1;
473	    p *= p;
474	    if (p > 0.55) p += 0.1 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 120));
475	    p = Math.min(1, p);
476	    scene.background.copy(SKY_SICK).lerp(SKY_VOID, p);
477	    scene.fog.color.copy(FOG_SICK).lerp(FOG_VOID, p);
478	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_SICK).lerp(DOME_VOID, p);
479	    sun.color.copy(SUN_SICK).lerp(SUN_VOID, p * 0.9);
480	  }
481	
482	  // ---------- briefing / fail / reset ----------
483	  function showBriefing() {
484	    fp.detach();
485	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
486	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
487	  }
488	  function startLevel() {
489	    if (started) return;
490	    started = true;
491	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
492	    fp.attach();
493	    showTutorial("The corruption is spreading from the nav panel's memory. Inspect the frozen memories (E) — their words are clues.", 8000);
494	  }
495	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
496	
497	  function failLevel() {
498	    if (failed || restored || restoring) return;
499	    failed = true;
500	    closeTerminal(false);
501	    closeClue();
502	    fp.detach();
503	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
504	    failEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
505	  }
506	  function resetLevel() {
507	    failed = false;
508	    restored = false;
509	    restoring = false;
510	    restoreT = 0;
511	    radius = START_RADIUS;
512	    searched = false;
513	    lastResults = [];
514	    explained.clear();
515	    consoleDismissed = false;
516	    for (const a of artifacts) a.inspected = false;
517	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
518	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
519	    updateMeter();
520	    if (active) fp.attach();
521	    showTutorial("New attempt — find the last good checkpoint before the corruption reaches the terminal.", 6000);
522	  }
523	
524	  // ---------- clue cards ----------
525	  function openClueCard(a) {
526	    openClue = a;
527	    fp.detach();
528	    if (!a.inspected) {
529	      a.inspected = true;
530	      updateMeter();
531	      if (a.clue.origin) {
532	        teachOnce("anomaly",
533	          `Anomaly time logged — ${ANOMALY}. When you explain a checkpoint, check its time against this.`, 8000);
534	      } else {
535	        teachOnce("clue",
536	          `Clue logged — “${a.clue.key}”. Words like this are searchable from the ship terminal.`, 7000);
537	      }
538	    }
539	    if (clueTitle) clueTitle.textContent = a.clue.title;
540	    if (clueBody) clueBody.textContent = a.clue.body;
541	    if (clueKey) clueKey.textContent = a.clue.key;
542	    clueCard?.classList.remove("hidden");
543	  }
544	  function closeClue() {
545	    if (!openClue) return;
546	    openClue = null;
547	    clueCard?.classList.add("hidden");
548	    if (active && !failed && !restored) fp.attach();
549	  }
550	
551	  // ---------- terminal ----------
552	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
553	    if (!termMsg) return;
554	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
555	    termMsg.textContent = text;
556	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
557	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
558	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 3600);
559	  }
560	
561	  // The next command the CTA should push the player toward.
562	  function suggestedCmd() {
563	    if (cluesFound() === 0) return null;
564	    if (!searched) {
565	      const firstClue = artifacts.find((a) => a.inspected && !a.clue.origin);
566	      return `entire checkpoint search "${firstClue ? firstClue.clue.key : "course plot"}"`;
567	    }
568	    if (explained.size === 0 && lastResults.length) {
569	      return `entire checkpoint explain ${lastResults[0].id}`;
570	    }
571	    return "entire checkpoint rewind";
572	  }
573	  function renderTerminal() {
574	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
575	    if (browserMode) return;   // the rewind browser owns the terminal until Esc/Enter
576	    if (confirmTarget) {
577	      termHint.textContent = "# rewinding is safe — you can always rewind again";
578	      termInput.textContent = `rewind to ${confirmTarget.id} (${confirmTarget.time})?  [y/n]`;
579	      termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
580	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
581	        `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
582	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd><span class="cta-note">rewind</span>` +
583	        `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-no">N</kbd><span class="cta-note">cancel</span>`;
584	      return;
585	    }
586	    termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
587	    termInput.textContent = buffer;
588	    const cmd = suggestedCmd();
589	    if (!cmd) {
590	      termHint.textContent = "# the log is searchable — but you need words to search for";
591	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
592	        `<span class="cta-label">FIRST</span>` +
593	        `<span class="cta-note">inspect the frozen memories — walk up, press E</span>`;
594	    } else {
595	      const why = !searched
596	        ? "Search the ship's checkpoint log for the words you found"
597	        : explained.size === 0
598	          ? "Explain a candidate — was it written before or after the anomaly?"
599	          : "Browse the checkpoints and rewind to the last good one";
600	      termHint.textContent = `# ${why}`;
601	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
602	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">${cmd}</span>`;
603	    }
604	  }
605	  function openTerminal() {
606	    if (terminalOpen) return;
607	    terminalOpen = true;
608	    buffer = "";
609	    fp.detach();
610	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
611	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
612	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
613	    teachOnce("terminal",
614	      "The ship terminal reads the checkpoint log — every memory you've ever banked. Search it, explain it, rewind it.", 7500);
615	    renderTerminal();
616	  }
617	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
618	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
619	    if (dismiss) consoleDismissed = true;
620	    terminalOpen = false;
621	    buffer = "";
622	    browserMode = false;
623	    confirmTarget = null;
624	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
625	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
626	    if (active && !failed && !restored && !restoring) fp.attach();
627	  }
628	
629	  // ---------- entire checkpoint search ----------
630	  function doSearch(raw) {
631	    const q = raw.replace(/^.*?search/i, "").replace(/["']/g, "").trim().toLowerCase();
632	    const words = q.split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length >= 3);
633	    if (!words.length) {
634	      flashTerminal('search needs words — try:  entire checkpoint search "course plot"', false);
635	      return;
636	    }
637	    const scored = CHECKPOINTS
638	      .map((cp) => ({
639	        cp,
640	        score: words.filter((w) => cp.summary.toLowerCase().includes(w)).length,
641	      }))
642	      .filter((r) => r.score > 0)
643	      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || (a.cp.time < b.cp.time ? 1 : -1));
644	    if (!scored.length) {
645	      flashTerminal(`0 results for “${q}” — try words from the memories you inspected`, false);
646	      return;
647	    }
648	    searched = true;
649	    lastResults = scored.map((r) => r.cp);
650	    if (termList) {
651	      termList.innerHTML = lastResults.map((cp) =>
652	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id">${cp.id}</span>` +
653	        `<span class="tl-time">${cp.time}</span>` +
654	        `<span class="tl-title">${cp.summary}</span></div>`
655	      ).join("");
656	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
657	    }
658	    flashTerminal(`${lastResults.length} checkpoint${lastResults.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} matched “${q}”`, true);
659	    teachOnce("searched",
660	      "Each result is a moment you can return to. `entire checkpoint explain <id>` shows what's inside one.", 8000);
661	  }
662	
663	  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain ----------
664	  function doExplain(n) {
665	    const token = n.replace(/^.*?explain/i, "").trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || "";
666	    if (token.length < 4) {
667	      flashTerminal("explain needs a checkpoint id — copy one from the search results", false);
668	      return;
669	    }
670	    const cp = CHECKPOINTS.find((c) => c.id.startsWith(token.toLowerCase()));
671	    if (!cp) {
672	      flashTerminal(`no checkpoint matching “${token}” — copy an id from the search results`, false);
673	      return;
674	    }
675	    explained.add(cp.id);
676	    if (termList) {
677	      const rows = [
678	        ["checkpoint", cp.id],
679	        ["time", cp.time],
680	        ["summary", cp.summary],
681	        ["files", cp.files],
682	        ["session", cp.session],
683	      ];
684	      termList.innerHTML = rows.map(([k, v]) =>
685	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">${k}</span>` +
686	        `<span class="tl-title">${v}</span></div>`
687	      ).join("") + (anomalyKnown()
688	        ? `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">#</span>` +
689	          `<span class="tl-anomaly">anomaly began ${ANOMALY} — is ${cp.time} before or after?</span></div>`
690	        : "");
691	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
692	    }
693	    flashTerminal(`explained ${cp.id}`, true);
694	    teachOnce("explained",
695	      "Explain shows the session behind a checkpoint — when, what, and why. Compare times. Then `entire checkpoint rewind`.", 8000);
696	  }
697	
698	  // ---------- entire checkpoint rewind ----------
699	  function openBrowser() {
700	    browserMode = true;
701	    browserSel = 0;
702	    renderBrowser();
703	    teachOnce("browser",
704	      "The rewind browser lists every checkpoint, newest first. ↑/↓ to choose, Enter to rewind, Esc to cancel.", 8000);
705	  }
706	  function renderBrowser() {
707	    termHint.textContent = "# entire checkpoint rewind — choose where to return to";
708	    termInput.textContent = "entire checkpoint rewind";
709	    termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
710	    if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
711	      `<span class="cta-label">KEYS</span>` +
712	      `<span class="cta-note">↑/↓ choose · Enter rewind · Esc cancel</span>`;
713	    if (termList) {
714	      termList.innerHTML = browserList().map((cp, i) =>
715	        `<div class="term-list-row${i === browserSel ? " is-sel" : ""}">` +
716	        `<span class="tl-sel">${i === browserSel ? "▸" : " "}</span>` +
717	        `<span class="tl-id">${cp.id}</span>` +
718	        `<span class="tl-time">${cp.time}</span>` +
719	        `<span class="tl-title">${cp.summary}</span></div>`
720	      ).join("");
721	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
722	    }
723	  }
724	  function applyRewind(cp) {
725	    confirmTarget = null;
726	    termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
727	    if (cp.good) {
728	      startRestore(cp);
729	      return;
730	    }
731	    // Soft fail — the wrong moment. The corruption surges, but rewinding is
732	    // safe and repeatable: that's the whole lesson.
733	    radius = Math.min(radius + SURGE, FAIL_RADIUS + 0.1);
734	    glitchFlash?.classList.remove("show");
735	    void glitchFlash?.offsetWidth;   // restart the flash animation
736	    glitchFlash?.classList.add("show");
737	    flashTerminal(
738	      `rewound to ${cp.id} — still corrupted. it was written ${cp.time}, after the anomaly. rewind again — rewinding is always safe.`,
739	      false
740	    );
741	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
742	    buffer = "";
743	    renderTerminal();
744	  }
745	  function startRestore(cp) {
746	    restoring = true;
747	    restoreT = 0;
748	    restoreFrom = radius;
749	    closeTerminal(false);
750	    fp.detach();
751	    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = `rewound to ${cp.id} · ${cp.summary}`;
752	    showTutorial(`Rewinding to ${cp.id} — ${cp.time}, the last good moment…`, 0);
753	  }
754	  function finishRestore() {
755	    restoring = false;
756	    restored = true;
757	    radius = 0;
758	    updateMeter();
759	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
760	    winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
761	    if (active) fp.attach();
762	  }
763	
764	  // ---------- command dispatch ----------
765	  function submitCommand() {
766	    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);
767	    buffer = "";
768	    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }
769	    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) search\b/.test(n)) doSearch(n);
770	    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) explain\b/.test(n)) doExplain(n);
771	    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) rewind\b/.test(n)) { openBrowser(); return; }
772	    else if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {
773	      if (termList) {
774	        termList.innerHTML = browserList().map((cp) =>
775	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id">${cp.id}</span>` +
776	          `<span class="tl-time">${cp.time}</span>` +
777	          `<span class="tl-title">${cp.summary}</span></div>`
778	        ).join("");
779	        termList.classList.remove("hidden");
780	      }
781	      flashTerminal("the full log — search finds the ones that matter", true);
782	    } else {
783	      const cmd = suggestedCmd();
784	      flashTerminal(`command not recognized${cmd ? ` — try:  ${cmd}` : " — inspect the memories for clues first"}`, false);
785	    }
786	    renderTerminal();
787	  }
788	
789	  // ---------- input ----------
790	  function onCanvasClick() {
791	    if (!started || failed) return;
792	    if (active && !fp.isLocked && !terminalOpen && !openClue) fp.lock();
793	  }
794	  function onKeyDown(e) {
795	    if (!active) return;
796	
797	    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
798	    if (!started) {
799	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
800	      return;
801	    }
802	
803	    // The corruption won — only R (retry) does anything.
804	    if (failed) {
805	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
806	      return;
807	    }
808	
809	    // Reading a clue — E or Esc puts it down.
810	    if (openClue) {
811	      if (e.code === "KeyE" || e.code === "Escape") { closeClue(); e.preventDefault(); }
812	      return;
813	    }
814	
815	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
816	    if (terminalOpen) {
817	      if (confirmTarget) {
818	        if (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y") { applyRewind(confirmTarget); e.preventDefault(); return; }
819	        if (e.key === "n" || e.key === "N" || e.code === "Escape") {
820	          confirmTarget = null;
821	          openBrowser();             // back to the browser, nothing lost
822	          e.preventDefault();
823	          return;
824	        }
825	        e.preventDefault();
826	        return;
827	      }
828	      if (browserMode) {
829	        const list = browserList();
830	        if (e.code === "ArrowUp") { browserSel = (browserSel + list.length - 1) % list.length; renderBrowser(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
831	        if (e.code === "ArrowDown") { browserSel = (browserSel + 1) % list.length; renderBrowser(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
832	        if (e.code === "Enter") {
833	          browserMode = false;
834	          confirmTarget = list[browserSel];
835	          termList?.classList.add("hidden");
836	          renderTerminal();
837	          e.preventDefault();
838	          return;
839	        }
840	        if (e.code === "Escape") {
841	          browserMode = false;
842	          termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
843	          termList?.classList.add("hidden");
844	          renderTerminal();
845	          e.preventDefault();
846	          return;
847	        }
848	        e.preventDefault();
849	        return;
850	      }
851	      if (e.code === "Escape") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
852	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
853	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
854	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
855	        buffer += e.key;
856	        renderTerminal();
857	        e.preventDefault();
858	      }
859	      return;
860	    }
861	
862	    // Walking around.
863	    if (e.code === "KeyE" && nearArtifact) { openClueCard(nearArtifact); e.preventDefault(); return; }
864	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
865	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
866	  }
867	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
868	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
869	
870	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
871	  let nearArtifact = null;
872	  let nearConsole = false;
873	
874	  function refreshHud() {
875	    if (!started || terminalOpen || openClue) {
876	      setPrompt(null);
877	      hideControls();
878	      return;
879	    }
880	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
881	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
882	    if (restored) { setPrompt("Timeline restored — press B to return to orbit"); return; }
883	    if (restoring) { setPrompt(null); return; }
884	    if (nearArtifact) {
885	      setPrompt(nearArtifact.inspected
886	        ? "Press E — review the clue"
887	        : "Press E — inspect the frozen memory");
888	      return;
889	    }
890	    const found = cluesFound();
891	    if (found === 0) setPrompt("Find the frozen memories — their words are clues");
892	    else if (found < artifacts.length) setPrompt("More clues remain — or head for the cyan beam: the ship terminal");
893	    else setPrompt("Head for the cyan beam — the ship terminal");
894	  }
895	
896	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
897	  function update(dt, t) {
898	    fp.update(dt);
899	
900	    // The corruption spreads while the run is live.
901	    if (active && started && !failed && !restoring && !restored) {
902	      radius += SPREAD_RATE * dt;
903	      if (radius >= FAIL_RADIUS) failLevel();
904	      updateMeter();
905	    }
906	
907	    // A good rewind pulls the wound back to nothing.
908	    if (restoring) {
909	      restoreT += dt;
910	      const k = Math.min(1, restoreT / RESTORE_DUR);
911	      radius = restoreFrom * (1 - k * k);   // accelerating retreat
912	      updateMeter();
913	      if (restoreT >= RESTORE_DUR) finishRestore();
914	    }
915	
916	    if (active) {
917	      paintCorruption(t);
918	      updateMotes(t);
919	      applySky();
920	    }
921	
922	    // Artifacts: gentle glow while safe; glitch-jitter once the wound has them.
923	    for (const a of artifacts) {
924	      const consumed = a.distFromOrigin <= radius + EDGE_BAND;
925	      const sick = a.clue.origin || consumed;
926	      if (sick && !restored && !restoring) {
927	        const j = a.clue.origin ? 0.22 : 0.12;
928	        a.ice.position.x = a.iceBaseX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * j;
929	        a.ice.position.y = a.iceBaseY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * j * 0.5;
930	        const blink = Math.sin(t * (a.clue.origin ? 14 : 9)) > 0;
931	        a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(blink ? 0xa6126e : 0x123a4a);
932	        a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.3;
933	        a.glow.color.setHex(blink ? 0xff2bd6 : 0x8fe3ff);
934	      } else {
935	        a.ice.position.x = a.iceBaseX;
936	        a.ice.position.y = a.iceBaseY;
937	        a.ice.material.emissive.setHex(0x2a6c8a);
938	        a.ice.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
939	        a.glow.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);
940	      }
941	    }
942	
943	    // The console screen flickers harder as the corruption closes in.
944	    const screen = consoleProp.userData.screen;
945	    if (screen) {
946	      const closeness = Math.min(1, radius / FAIL_RADIUS);
947	      const flick = Math.sin(t * (6 + closeness * 18)) > -0.2 + closeness * 0.8;
948	      screen.material.emissive.setHex(
949	        restored || restoring ? 0x2a8aa6 : flick ? 0x2a8aa6 : 0xa6126e
950	      );
951	    }
952	
953	    // Proximity: nearest inspectable memory, and the ship terminal.
954	    nearArtifact = null;
955	    let nd = Infinity;
956	    for (const a of artifacts) {
957	      const d = Math.hypot(
958	        camera.position.x - a.anchor.position.x,
959	        camera.position.z - a.anchor.position.z
960	      );
961	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; if (d <= INTERACT_DIST) nearArtifact = a; }
962	    }
963	    nearConsole = Math.hypot(
964	      camera.position.x - CONSOLE_POS.x, camera.position.z - CONSOLE_POS.z
965	    ) <= CONSOLE_DIST;
966	
967	    // The terminal opens itself when you reach the console, like Level 1.
968	    if (active && started && !failed && !restoring && !restored && !openClue) {
969	      if (consoleDismissed && !nearConsole) consoleDismissed = false;
970	      if (nearConsole && !terminalOpen && !consoleDismissed) openTerminal();
971	      else if (!nearConsole && terminalOpen) closeTerminal(false);
972	    }
973	
974	    if (terminalOpen && !browserMode && !confirmTarget) renderTerminal();
975	    if (active) refreshHud();
976	  }
977	
978	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
979	  function enter() {
980	    active = true;
981	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
982	    camera.lookAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z) + 5, CONSOLE_POS.z);
983	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
984	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
985	    l2Hud?.classList.remove("hidden");
986	    fpShared?.classList.remove("hidden");
987	    setControls(false);
988	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
989	    updateMeter();
990	    if (!started) {
991	      showBriefing();
992	    } else if (restored) {
993	      fp.attach();
994	      showTutorial("Timeline restored — press B to return to orbit.", 0);
995	    } else if (failed) {
996	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
997	    } else {
998	      fp.attach();
999	      showTutorial("The corruption never stopped. Find the last good checkpoint.", 6000);
1000	    }
1001	  }
1002	  function exit() {
1003	    active = false;
1004	    closeTerminal(false);
1005	    closeClue();
1006	    fp.unlock();
1007	    fp.detach();
1008	    canvas.removeEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
1009	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
1010	    setPrompt(null);
1011	    hideControls();
1012	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1013	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1014	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1015	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1016	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
1017	    clueCard?.classList.add("hidden");
1018	    l2Hud?.classList.add("hidden");
1019	    fpShared?.classList.add("hidden");
1020	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
1021	  }
1022	  function resize() {
1023	    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
1024	    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
1025	  }
1026	
1027	  return { scene, camera, update, enter, exit, resize };
1028	}
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { PointerLockControls } from "three/addons/controls/PointerLockControls.js";
3	
4	// First-person walker: PointerLockControls for mouse-look + keyboard movement
5	// that hugs the terrain. Click the canvas to look around; Escape releases the mouse.
6	
7	export function createFirstPerson(camera, domElement, {
8	  heightAt,
9	  radius = 80,        // keep the walker on the island, not out at sea
10	  eyeHeight = 2.4,
11	  speed = 22,         // world units / second
12	} = {}) {
13	  const controls = new PointerLockControls(camera, domElement);
14	
15	  const keys = { f: false, b: false, l: false, r: false };
16	  function onKey(down) {
17	    return (e) => {
18	      switch (e.code) {
19	        case "KeyW": case "ArrowUp": keys.f = down; break;
20	        case "KeyS": case "ArrowDown": keys.b = down; break;
21	        case "KeyA": case "ArrowLeft": keys.l = down; break;
22	        case "KeyD": case "ArrowRight": keys.r = down; break;
23	        default: return;
24	      }
25	      // Don't let arrow keys scroll the page while walking.
26	      if (down) e.preventDefault();
27	    };
28	  }
29	  const onKeyDown = onKey(true);
30	  const onKeyUp = onKey(false);
31	
32	  // Place the walker on the ground at a given (x, z).
33	  function groundAt(x, z) {
34	    camera.position.set(x, heightAt(x, z) + eyeHeight, z);
35	  }
36	
37	  function update(dt) {
38	    if (controls.isLocked) {
39	      const d = speed * dt;
40	      // Forward/back and strafe are horizontal; height is re-applied below.
41	      if (keys.f) controls.moveForward(d);
42	      if (keys.b) controls.moveForward(-d);
43	      if (keys.r) controls.moveRight(d);
44	      if (keys.l) controls.moveRight(-d);
45	
46	      // Clamp to the island so you can't walk off into the sea.
47	      const p = camera.position;
48	      const dist = Math.hypot(p.x, p.z);
49	      if (dist > radius) {
50	        const s = radius / dist;
51	        p.x *= s; p.z *= s;
52	      }
53	    }
54	    // Always glue the eye to the terrain (even while standing still after a
55	    // teleport/reset), so the ground never clips through the camera.
56	    camera.position.y = heightAt(camera.position.x, camera.position.z) + eyeHeight;
57	  }
58	
59	  function attach() {
60	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
61	    window.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
62	  }
63	  function detach() {
64	    window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
65	    window.removeEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
66	    keys.f = keys.b = keys.l = keys.r = false;
67	  }
68	
69	  return {
70	    controls,
71	    update,
72	    groundAt,
73	    attach,
74	    detach,
75	    lock: () => controls.lock(),
76	    unlock: () => controls.unlock(),
77	    get isLocked() { return controls.isLocked; },
78	  };
79	}
80
```
